主纤维定理的完整 Lean 类型（本轮新声明）：

```lean
iutNegLog_norm_embedding_sum_liesOver.{u}
    {F K : Type u} [Field F] [NumberField F]
    [Field K] [NumberField K] [Algebra F K]
    (x : F) (w : NumberField.FinitePlace F)
    (s : Finset (NumberField.FinitePlace K))
    (hs : ∀ v, v ∈ s ↔
      Ideal.LiesOver v.maximalIdeal.asIdeal w.maximalIdeal.asIdeal) :
    (∑ v ∈ s, -Real.log ‖NumberField.FinitePlace.embedding v.maximalIdeal
      (algebraMap F K x)‖) =
      (Module.finrank F K : ℝ) *
        (-Real.log ‖NumberField.FinitePlace.embedding w.maximalIdeal x‖)
```

JoshiTeich 适配与独立工程：本轮只使用已在本工程重新声明和证明的局部 completion、
有限处与 Galois 下降 API。没有复制新 IUT 文件，也没有把 `refer/iut` 加入依赖。
这些局部算术恒等式可复用；IUT 的 degree-weighted q-pilot 不自动等于 Joshi III 的
未加权代表处和。新的完整纤维等式也不识别论文的代表选择。

## S1.239：canonical Tate 字段到实际 q-pilot source

时间：2026-10-13 02:28 CST（Asia/Shanghai；最终验证时间在下方追加）

上一目标轮分类：progress。`fa7d9e9` 已在 `origin/main`；工作树开始时干净。
当前行动：检查真实 `InitialThetaData` 与 canonical link 的现有投影，发现 canonical
adapter 已证明参数身份。因而将本轮定位为字段来源收紧：不只增加审计投影，还把
同源参数等式交给实际 q-pilot source 构造器，取消独立 carrier/逐处参数/j 证书。

实际闭合程度：

- 闭合：真实 base source 与同一初始数据 canonical link 的逐处参数等式，以及从
  该等式推出的 q-order 等式。以本条最终窄构建成功输出为验证依据。
- 新增：`IutQPilotSource.ofInitialThetaCanonicalTateData`。从 `hEll`、
  `hTate : T.scene1.data.link.tate = iutInitialThetaTateParameterData D` 和
  `hCompat : QNormTateCompatibility T.scene1.data.link` 装配实际 q-pilot source。
  新生产模块与 `Audit/CanonicalSourceAudit.lean`；唯一 source-id
  `IUT-COR312-CANONICAL-SOURCE-FIELD-AUDIT`；公开 Scene1 与完整 ImportAudit 接入。
- 退休：仅退休新构造路径上独立 `hPlaces`、逐处 `hParameter` 和 `hLinkTateJ` 的
  输入义务。旧公开定理和构造器的类型保留，未删除这些通用接口。
- 转入 Prologue：无。
- 从 Prologue 取出：无。边界保持 `Prologue-R5 → Prologue-R5`。

边界记录：`S1-DERIVED-239`，operation `derive`，状态 `accepted` 为
Scene1/Derived。source-id 如上；owner：Scene1；审查者：本轮动态边界审计。
本轮没有新增 Core 或 Candidate。声明的完整类型见以下签名与源码中的两个投影
定理；它们均有完整 proof term。

```lean
IutQPilotSource.ofInitialThetaCanonicalTateData
    {AG : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.AnabelianGeometry.{u}}
    {TG : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.TemperedGeometry AG}
    (D : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.InitialThetaData AG TG)
    {O : Type u} [CommRing O] (p : ℕ)
    [Fact p.Prime] [Fact (¬ IsUnit (p : O))]
    [IsAdicComplete (Ideal.span {(p : O)}) O]
    {T : Corollary312Data D.F O p}
    (hEll : T.ell = D.prime.ℓ)
    (hTate : T.scene1.data.link.tate = iutInitialThetaTateParameterData D)
    (hCompat : QNormTateCompatibility T.scene1.data.link) :
    IutQPilotSource p T
```

来源与模型：参数两边都投影实际 `D.prime.tate`；有限 carrier 来自已证明的
`iutInitialThetaTateParameterData_places_eq` 与 `hCompat.places_eq`。
满足证据为现有 canonical `AdelicThetaLinkData.ofInitialThetaData D ...` 的
Tate projection 和 q-norm compatibility；仍需显式 `T` 与其来源等式，未构造真实
完整 `Corollary312Scene1QPilotData`。一般 link 不能自动满足 Tate projection
身份；若其 carrier 或参数与 `D` 不同，就不能使用本构造器。当前原理不增加
`Nonempty InitialThetaData` 假设，不向全局提供隐藏构造或实例。

生产者与消费者：参数等式被新构造器的 `hParameter` 消费，并传给现有
`ofInitialThetaData_of_parameter_eq`；后者通过 j 唯一性和实际 admissible-prime
uniformizer、阶互素证书装配 source。q-order 投影单独由窄公理审计检查。
依赖图仅增加新叶子模块及其窄审计；公开聚合入口增加 import。预期影响为新模块、
窄审计与两个聚合入口；本轮不构建聚合入口。现有高扇出数学声明及工具链未改。

数学负担变化：新路径的 Scene1 carrier、参数和 j 识别负担由 proof term 闭合；
`hTate` 和 `hCompat` 仍须由真实目标来源满足。前置信任负担不变，没有把 3.12
不等式或等价体积下界加入 Prologue。该变化不等价于 Corollary312，删除它只会
恢复调用者的字段识别工作。本轮不使用 degree-one 条件解决一般数域的归一化问题。

JoshiTeich 理论适配：III `ss:theta-data-fixing2` 与
`pr:admissible-lifts-tate-params` 的 Tate 参数作用与本来源识别相容；本轮没有
构造 Fargues–Fontaine 商映射、untilt、theta lift 或论文 `L'` 代表处。
IUT 已移植的局部 Tate API 仅作为本项目证明的基础。本轮没有复制新参考文件，
没有把参考工程加入导入图、子模块或 Lake 依赖。

数学类型（用缩写列出，源码给出完整 universe/instance 类型）：

```lean
D : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.InitialThetaData AG TG
T : Corollary312Data D.F O p
hTate : T.scene1.data.link.tate = iutInitialThetaTateParameterData D
hCompat : QNormTateCompatibility T.scene1.data.link
hEll : T.ell = D.prime.ℓ
B := IutBaseQPilotSource.ofInitialThetaData D
R := IutTorsionQRootSource.ofInitialThetaDataFromGlobal D
v : R.badFinset
w := (R.basePlace v).1
i := CanonicalQRootNorm.baseIndex D p hTate hCompat v
e := Arithmetic.relRamIdx v.1 w
f := Arithmetic.relInertDeg v.1 w

‖R.qroot v‖ ^ (2 * T.ell) = T.scene1.data.link.qNorm w ^ (e * f)
-Real.log ‖R.qroot v‖ = (e * f : ℝ) * (-Real.log (Corollary312SourceQFactor p T i))
‖R.qroot v‖ = Corollary312SourceQFactor p T i ^ (e * f)

CanonicalQRootNorm.paperRootNorm D v := ‖R.qroot v‖ ^ (1 / (Arithmetic.inertDeg v.1 : ℝ))
-Real.log (CanonicalQRootNorm.paperRootNorm D v) =
  (e : ℝ) * (-Real.log (Corollary312PaperNormalizedSourceQFactor p T i))
```

理论适配：当前 completion norm 是有限处绝对范数，uniformizer 范数含绝对
剩余次数；它不等同于 Joshi III 的 paper-normalized 局部范数。局部转换沿已登记的
`PaperQNormalizationBridge` 进行；未以抹去 `e`、`f` 或加入 degree-one 条件来
替代归一化问题。证明只适用于实际 `badPlacesOver(D.VBad)` 的 base support 及
其全部 torsion-field 上方 carrier，不把它宣称为论文的独立 `L'` representatives。
本轮不复制新 IUT 代码，不增加外部依赖，保留独立工程原则。

## S1.241：canonical InitialTheta Scene1 input boundary（2026-10-13）

本轮恢复 Act1Scene1 数学工作，处理“真实 `InitialThetaData/TateFamily` 满足模型”中的
输入边界子单元。新增 `CanonicalInitialThetaScene1Input`，由同一个实际
`InitialThetaData` 构造 canonical `AdelicThetaLinkData.ofInitialThetaData`，并提供
`toScene1Input` 与 `toData`。因此 Scene1 的 finite-place carrier、局部 Tate 参数、q-parameter
和 `j` 来源固定在 canonical link 上，不能再由一个独立 link 加 equality certificate 脱钩。

本单元没有构造 Perfectoid bridge、theta lift、packet/hull、volume transfer、论文
`V_p^oss`/`L'` representatives 或最终 q-family accounting；这些仍在结构中作为显式义务。
因此 §2.1 的真实满足模型复选框、§3 四个形式冻结闸门和最终 Corollary 3.12 复选框保持未勾选。
没有 Prologue 边界变更：`Prologue-R5 → Prologue-R5`；Scene1 proof burden 减少为在
canonical link 上完成剩余来源义务，前置信任 burden 不增加。没有新增、退休或转入
Prologue 的数学声明。


## S1.243：真实 q-root 到 theta log-shell 的符号审计（2026-10-13）

上一轮是有验证、提交和推送的进展。本轮从真实 theta 满足模型继续核查，发现
当前正 log-volume contract 与直接 q-root norm 装配不相容。新模块
`ThetaLogSignAudit.lean` 提供四个 proof terms：输入 transfer 的非负性、theta base
范数至少为 1、实际 canonical q-root 范数小于 1、以及两者不相等。

证明直接使用当前 Source 数据：根的 completionMap 是 canonical embedding；
真实 root power 映到范数小于 1 的 `D.prime.tate` 参数，由幂的单调性得到根范数
小于 1。对 Scene1，theta scaling 使所有 norm 在 base norm 小于 1 时至多为 1，
base 处对数严格负；有限求和和 period/log-shell 等式使 logVolume 严格负，
与 positive packet/hull transfer 的非负性矛盾。因此可以排除该装配路径。

本轮读到 Joshi III 原文 `th:adelic-theta-link`（1139–1154）：`j²` 缩放是不同
untilt residue fields 的估值关系。既有 valuation_scaling 字段
`‖q‖^(j*j) = ‖q^(j*j)‖` 是普通 norm power 法律，不能被当作这项几何关系的证明。
3634–3650 的原文将非正 `logVol` 与非负 `|logVol|` 区分；本工程当前 positive
logVolume 不可在没有来源转换时直接当成普通 `log(norm)`。这校正了上一轮
“valuation scaling 已推导”的适配范围：仅当前字段的代数法律通过，论文关系仍开放。

### 动态边界记录 S1-BND-243

| 字段 | 记录 |
| --- | --- |
| revision / operation | `Prologue-R5 → Prologue-R5`；`derive` 与装配路径 `reject`，不修改 Core |
| declaration / statement | `Scene1Input.one_le_thetaBase_norm (S : Scene1Input K O p) : 1 ≤ S.data.link.theta.norm S.data.link.thetaBase`；`ofInitialThetaDataFromGlobal_qroot_norm_lt_one (D : InitialThetaData AG TG) (v : (ofInitialThetaDataFromGlobal D).badFinset) : ‖(ofInitialThetaDataFromGlobal D).qroot v‖ < 1`；`Scene1Input.thetaBase_norm_ne_canonical_qroot_norm S D v : S.data.link.theta.norm S.data.link.thetaBase ≠ ‖(ofInitialThetaDataFromGlobal D).qroot v‖`；标准实例与完整类型见 Lean 源码 |
| target-role / sources / fidelity | 排除直接 root norm theta 装配；`JT-SCENE1-THETA-LOG-SIGN-AUDIT`，Joshi III 上述来源与既有 IUT root source；不证明不同 untilt 估值关系 |
| proof-status / owner | 新证明最终窄构建验证后为 proved；真实 theta 模型与符号转换仍 open；owner Scene1 |
| model-evidence | 实际 `D` 的所有 root places 都有严格单位球结论；当前 S 的 base 范数至少 1；条件性全模型存在性未证明 |
| dependency-effect | 新 Scene1 窄模块；SceneBounds check +4、公理 +3；Act1Scene1 聚合导入；不导入 Obligations/Candidate/Scene2/Act2 |

## S1.244：实际 q-root 的归一化 theta witness 乘积估计（2026-10-13）

上一轮 S1.243 有新增 proof terms、窄验证和推送，属于进展。本轮沿其负面证据阅读
Joshi III 的源证明，定位到真正用于局部乘积下界的 `j²/ellStar²`，而非把未归一化
`j²` 和原始 q-root norm 填回旧 `ThetaPilotScalingLaws`。权重范围在
`ss:weighted-vol`、`le:volume-lemma2` 和 `le:volume-lemma3` 明确为 `(0,1]`，
使单位球内半径的加权实数幂不小于原半径。

新模块 `ThetaWitnessProduct.lean` 定义归一化 square scale 与有限乘积，证明
其正性、`a^n` 下界、1 上界、负对数精确有限和公式，以及非负的 log-cost 上界。
然后以实际 `InitialThetaData` 的 `CanonicalQRootNorm.paperRootNorm` 为半径、
实际 `(ell-1)/2` 为长度装配局部 witness 估计。真实 root 非零与严格单位球
结论已消费，不需脱钩范数证明。

## S1.245：实际 q-root 完成域中的局部对数见证（2026-10-13）

本单元闭合普通 p-adic logarithm 的局部解析前提。`LocalPadicLog.lean` 独立移植
参考工程的整数范数、级数收敛和小球余项证明，再证明
`‖padicLog (1+a)‖=‖a‖`（`‖a‖≤‖2p‖`）。
`IutInitialThetaLogWitness.lean` 从实际处的 residue characteristic 派生
`‖p‖<1`，对实际 canonical q-root `y` 构造 `u=1+2*p*y` 和
`z=(2*p)⁻¹*padicLog u`。证明主单位条件、log-shell 成员、`‖z‖=‖y‖` 和非零性。

移植源为 `refer/iut/Iut/Concrete/LocalConstruct/PadicLog.lean`，选定段为参考快照
49–157、161–243、425–494 行；原始提交
`bc1c6e6c7ac4bcf0c363b9b1bbe9b6d7bc35bf9c`，快照
`07cb1da58f6d428a670dbb5dd6093d428bcbcdd8`。Git 作者 Christian Merten，
文件作者 The iut contributors；复制前核查文件头和双方 Apache-2.0 LICENSE，
保留版权及许可证通知。新模块使用独立命名空间和精确导入，不引入参考工程依赖。
本轮未复制 logarithm 同态、满射、紧性或 tensor-packet 构造。

## S1.246：局部对数满射与实际来源整格球（2026-10-13 11:28 CST）

上一轮已经推送 `2b0c116`，新 proof terms、实际来源消费者及审计均通过，属于实际
进展。本轮核对 Joshi III `le:volume-lemma2`（3531–3543）：局部体积估计使用
`lambda*O_E` 整格区域，需要完整集合而不只是一个 logarithm 见证。

`LocalPadicLogBall.lean` 独立移植参考文件的 496–529 行小球满射证明。映射
`a ↦ y-logRem a` 在 `‖2p‖` 闭球上为 contraction，由 Banach 不动点定理构造
`padicLog(1+a)=y`。新增证明将其提升为 normalizedLog 的单位球映满等式，进而
得到整个单位球包含于普通 log-shell。

`IutInitialThetaLogBall.lean` 用实际 S1.245 logarithm 见证 `z=value D v`
定义 `integralBall D v = z*{x : ‖x‖≤1}`。证明该实际集合等于半径为实际
canonical q-root 范数的闭球，包含 `z`，位于普通 log-shell；任意包含 `z` 的
零中心范数球也包含它。这给出局部标量整格及其在零中心范数球中的最小性，
不识别它为论文 tensor hull 或实际 thetaVolume。

移植源 `refer/iut/Iut/Concrete/LocalConstruct/PadicLog.lean`，原始提交
`bc1c6e6c7ac4bcf0c363b9b1bbe9b6d7bc35bf9c`，参考快照

## S1.247：实际完成域的局部紧致与 Haar 整格球测度（2026-10-13 11:53 CST）

上一轮 S1.246 已推送 `2ee1ae3`。本轮处理论文 `le:volume-lemma1` 所需的局部正有限测度区域。参考工程的 `Packet.lean` 证明实际 number-field completion 具有 `ℚ_p` 连续嵌入和有限维性；`Integral.lean` 与 `LogShell.lean` 给出整数球相对紧和完成域 ProperSpace。选定证明段已独立移植，文件头、来源提交和 Apache-2.0 通知已登记。

新增 `LocalCompletionHaar.lean`：用实际单位闭球构造 `PositiveCompacts`，定义单位球测度为 1 的 `normalizedHaar`，并证明任意非零标量球的测度等于 Mathlib `distribHaarChar`。新增 `IutInitialThetaLogBallHaar.lean` 消费实际 q-root 对数整格球，证明其紧、开，并具有严格正且有限的 Haar 测度。缩放因子明确保留为 `distribHaarChar`，尚未证明它等于 completion norm。

## S1.248：局部主理想指数与实际整格球测度（2026-10-13）

上一个已推送单元是 S1.247（`d9fd48c`）。本单元沿实际 Haar measure 到
论文范数识别的路线推进，使用紧致整数环上的有限陪集计数。新文件
`LocalCompletionHaarIndex.lean` 构造实际完成域整数环的 comap 测度，证明
非零整元素主理想开、闭且加法指数有限；由 Mathlib
`AddSubgroup.index_mul_measure` 得到标量整格球测度为指数倒数。
`IutInitialThetaLogBallIndex.lean` 固定实际 canonical q-root 的对数见证，
证明整性、非零性、指数正性、实际球测度公式及测度不超过 1。

## S1.249：实际局部商同构与 Haar/norm 识别（2026-10-13 15:01 CST）

上一轮 S1.248 已推送 `bfc7da3`。本轮推进其有限指数到 actual completion norm
的识别：给出全局整数到实际完成域主理想商的映射，证明满射并计算核，从而
把未知商基数消去。`LocalCompletionHaarNorm.lean` 先用完成域的全局域稠密性
逼近任一整数元素，再用 Mathlib `exists_valuation_sub_lt_of_integer` 将该域
近似换成全局整数。以非零整元素 `z` 的范数作半径，两次近似由 ultrametric
合并，产生全局整数到 `integerRing w ⧸ (z)` 的满射。

若 `Valued.v z=exp(-n)`，核恰是全局处理想的 n 次幂；第一同构定理得到实际
商环同构。商基数为 `absNorm(w)^n`，配合 S1.248 的 Haar 指数公式及现有
completion valuation norm 公式，得到实际标量整格球的实值测度等于原始完成域
范数。`IutInitialThetaLogBallNorm.lean` 固定实际 canonical q-root 对数见证，
从 completionQOrder 派生整数阶，目标为实际整格球测度等于 `ENNReal.ofReal ‖qroot‖`。

## S1.251：实际张量整格的紧致开放性与正有限 Haar 测度（2026-10-13）

上一轮 S1.250 已推送 `94679f2`，真实 tensor carrier 和非零 logarithm 见证
已实现。本轮处理 §2.3 的实际 tensor region 拓扑前提。适配参考
`Packet.lean` 基坐标 norm/topology，以及 `Integral.lean` 的有界 span、
缩放因子基再重组张量基、开放整格的路线；原始提交和 Apache-2.0 通知完整
登记于新文件头与来源账本，未引入参考工程依赖。

`LocalCompletionTensorTopology.lean` 为 S1.250 的 actual tensor carrier
建立 finite-dimensional `ℚ_p` normed topology，证明 unitLattice 有界。
对任意正的 factor radii，先证明某个 `p^M` 缩放的 tensor basis 位于生成
整格，再由 basis coordinates 构造 contained closed ball。由此整格是开
加法子群、也是闭子群；当半径≤1时它有界，因此紧致。实际 tensor Haar
measure 由 Mathlib 构造，非空开放区域测度正、紧区域测度有限。
`IutInitialThetaTensorRegion.lean` 用 canonical q-root 半径实例化全部条件。

## S1.252：实际张量缩放像与整格归一化 Haar 公式（2026-10-13）

上一轮 S1.251 已推送 `196d596`，实际张量区域具有compact-open与正有限测度。
本轮构造逐因子乘非零标量的真实线性自同构：`LocalCompletionTensorScale.lean`
使用 `PiTensorProduct.congr`，并证明它的单位整格像恰为标量整格。有限维
拓扑使它成为continuous linear equivalence。单位整格compact-open证据用于
构造测度归一为1的Haar measure，真实scaled lattice的测度等于该自同构的
Haar分布特征。

`IutInitialThetaTensorScale.lean` 把标量固定为实际canonical q-root logarithm，
证明此前region是该continuous scale的单位整格像，测度精确公式和正且≤1。
本轮不把未计算的Haar特征当作factor norm乘积，亦不把unitLattice当作论文
maximal order或hull。论文 `le:volume-lemma2/3` 的标量整格路线仍是目标来源。

## S1.264：实际 factor 乘法矩阵与域范数（2026-10-13 21:12:57 CST）

上一轮 S1.263 已推送 `3b64eb3`；本轮补齐 factor multiplication matrix 与实际
`Algebra.norm ℚ_[p]` 的连接，并把 tensor-scale 矩阵项写成实际 factor 矩阵项的
乘积。`factorMulMatrix` 定义为实际 completion 的有限基中的 `Algebra.leftMulMatrix`，
不需要先假设非零；其 determinant 等于 field norm，非零标量给出非零 determinant，
来自 `ℚ_[p]` 的标量给出 `a ^ Module.finrank`。actual module 固定 canonical log 值，
生成 `factorLogMulMatrix` 与 `factorLogNorm`，并消费 generic 公式。

这里的 `Algebra.norm` 是域扩张的代数范数，值仍在 `ℚ_[p]` 中；不能将它直接
等同于 completion 的实数范数 `‖x‖`、paper-normalized norm 或 Haar character。
本轮尚未证明 entire tensor determinant 的 factor-norm 幂乘积或测度公式。

### 动态边界记录 S1-BND-264

| 字段 | 记录 |
| --- | --- |
| revision / operation | `Prologue-R5 → Prologue-R5`；`derive`，Core 不变 |
| declaration / statement | 对实际 `P`、`i : ι`、`x : P.Factor i`，`factorMulMatrix i x : Matrix (Fin (Module.finrank ℚ_[p] (P.Factor i))) (Fin (Module.finrank ℚ_[p] (P.Factor i))) ℚ_[p] := Algebra.leftMulMatrix (Module.finBasis ℚ_[p] (P.Factor i)) x`；`factorMulMatrix_apply i x k l : P.factorMulMatrix i x k l = (Module.finBasis ℚ_[p] (P.Factor i)).repr (x * Module.finBasis ℚ_[p] (P.Factor i) l) k`；`factorMulMatrix_det_eq_norm : (P.factorMulMatrix i x).det = Algebra.norm ℚ_[p] x`；`factorMulMatrix_det_ne_zero i x (hx : x ≠ 0) : (P.factorMulMatrix i x).det ≠ 0`；`factorMulMatrix_det_algebraMap i a : (P.factorMulMatrix i (algebraMap ℚ_[p] (P.Factor i) a)).det = a ^ Module.finrank ℚ_[p] (P.Factor i)`；`scaleMatrix_eq_factorMul_prod z hz k l : P.scaleMatrix z hz k l = ∏ i, P.factorMulMatrix i (z i) (k i) (l i)`；actual `factorLogMulMatrix` 与 `factorLogNorm` 固定 `z i = InitialThetaLogWitness.value D (c i)`，并证明 `factorLogMulMatrix_det_eq_norm`、`factorLogMulMatrix_det_ne_zero`、`scaleMatrix_eq_factorLogMul_prod`；完整参数见两个 ScaleMatrix 文件 |

## S1.265：完整张量行列式的 factor-norm 幂乘积（2026-10-13 21:25:54 CST）

上一轮 S1.264 已推送 `d248fbe`；本轮将完整 tensor-scale 行列式展开为 actual
factor algebraic norms 的幂乘积。新 `FiniteTensorMatrixDeterminant.lean` 在任意
交换环上证明 finite tensor matrix 的行列式公式，通过有限类型的 empty/option
归纳，将一个新 factor 用 `Equiv.piOptionEquivProd` 拆出，再调用 Mathlib
`Matrix.det_kronecker`。指数定义为“其余因子维数之积”，不使用可能含零维分母
的 `totalDimension / factorDimension`。空 tuple 的 tensor basis index 是单点，
行列式为 1；零维 factor 也在同一公式中处理。

通用 completion consumer 将 S1.264 的 factor matrices 代入该定理，用实际
`factorMulMatrix_det_eq_norm` 替换每个 determinant；actual consumer 固定
canonical log scalars 与 source completion 的 p-adic algebra instance。本轮
没有 IUT 代码移植、升级依赖或修改 Mathlib。

## S1.266：p-adic Haar 的维数缩放与真实 tensor region（2026-10-13 21:45:08 CST）

上一轮 S1.265 已推送 `6665e1f`，完整 tensor determinant 已计算为 factor
algebraic norm 的幂乘积。冻结 Mathlib 的现成 general Haar/determinant 公式
在 `MeasureTheory/Measure/Lebesgue` 中要求实向量空间，不能直接用在 `ℚ_[p]`。
本轮建立其中缺少的 prime-scalar Haar 归一化步骤，并应用于真实 region：
`μ(p • region) = p^(-dim tensor) * μ(region)`，指数再由 S1.261 展开成 actual
completion dimensions 之积，并接入 S1.253 的实际 finite-index 测度公式。
这提供一般 determinant/Haar 连接的标量步骤；未将其误作任意自同构的结论。

逐文件适配 `refer/iut/Iut/Concrete/LocalConstruct/Haar.lean`（原提交
`4d2b6e926089b949109c69c28f13c7f878249c5d`，Git 作者 Christian Merten，文件
作者 The iut contributors，快照 `07cb1da58f6d428a670dbb5dd6093d428bcbcdd8`）。
复制前检查文件版权头与双方 LICENSE，均为 Apache-2.0；本地文件保留版权与
许可证通知、路径、作者、原提交及改写范围。只移植 finite coset counting 和
finite coordinate Haar transport 证明，改为本地 `JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1.PadicHaar`
命名空间及精确 imports，未导入参考工程、packet 或 reference volume。

## S1.272：代表纤维 mass-one 条件的限制（2026-10-14 00:55:13 CST）

上一 goal turn S1.271 为 progress，`f9b5661` 已推送。开始时当前工作树干净，
`main` 与 `origin/main` 同步。按 Scene1 2.2 的 paper representative/weight
会计义务，检查当前 `Corollary312QPilotPaperRepresentativeAccounting`：该结构
要求每个 representative fibre 的实际 `Arithmetic.qPilotWeight` 和为 1。
Core 已证明每个 rational prime 下全部 source q-pilot weights 的和至多 1。
因此新单元验证这个去权条件对真实代表选择的必要限制，而非补充自由证书。

新增窄叶子 `PaperQRepresentativeMassAudit.lean`，只精确导入现有 representative
/ selected-q-root bridge。三条公共定理构成消费链：mass-one 先推出 source map
满射；加上显式 prime preservation 后，证明代表 prime label 单射；最后通过
actual `InitialThetaData` section-link 的已有 `representativeLinkEquiv`，得到
section-link 处的 residue characteristic 单射。这严格排除该条件在保持 prime
的同时覆盖同一 rational prime 下两个不同代表，但未提供实际 two-place
countermodel，也未从独立 source map 推出 prime preservation。

`qPilotRepresentativeFiberMass` 为 actual arithmetic weights 的 fibre sum，

q-pilot 的 weighted representative sum 等式和 nonnegative unweighted sum 上界。
weight、mass 都由现有数域/有限处 source 派生，未额外接收 mass 字段。

representative term 的非负性保持显式：空 fibre mass 为 0，不要求 representative
map 满射，也不替空 fibre 任意宣称一个负 term 为非负。没有改变旧 certificate
或 current Corollary312 的类型，也不把上界当成 literal paper equality。

S1-BND-280 公共声明完整 Lean 类型（与生产文件同一精确导入及实例环境）：

```lean
namespace JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1
open JoshiTeich.Prologue
open scoped BigOperators Classical
universe u
noncomputable section
variable {AG : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.AnabelianGeometry.{u}}
variable {TG : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.TemperedGeometry AG}

def iutInitialThetaModuliSelectedSourceEquiv
    (D : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.InitialThetaData AG TG) :
    (iutInitialThetaOddModSupportFinset D) ≃
      {s : (iutInitialThetaOddSupportTorsionQRootSource D).badFinset //
        s ∈ iutInitialThetaOddSupportSelectedQRootSourcePlaces D}

theorem iutInitialThetaModuliJLogTerm_eq_weightedBaseQOrder
    (D : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.InitialThetaData AG TG)
    (v : NumberField.FinitePlace ↥(JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.fieldOfModuli D.F D.E))
    (hv : v ∈ iutInitialThetaOddModSupportFinset D) :
    let hv' := (iutInitialThetaOddModSupport_finite D).mem_toFinset.mp hv
    let R := iutInitialThetaOddSupportTorsionQRootSource D
    let B := iutInitialThetaOddSupportBaseQPilotSource D
    let s := iutInitialThetaOddSupportSelectedQRootPlace D v hv'
    let b := R.basePlace s
    Real.log ‖NumberField.FinitePlace.embedding v.maximalIdeal
      (⟨D.E.j, IntermediateField.mem_adjoin_simple_self ℚ D.E.j⟩ :
        ↥(JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.fieldOfModuli D.F D.E))‖ /
      (2 * (D.prime.ℓ : ℝ) *
        (Module.finrank ℚ ↥(JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.fieldOfModuli D.F D.E) : ℝ)) =
      (Arithmetic.qPilotWeight v / (Arithmetic.relRamIdx b.1 v : ℝ)) *
        ((B.qOrder b : ℝ) / (2 * (D.prime.ℓ : ℝ))) *
          Real.log (Arithmetic.residueChar b.1)

theorem iutInitialThetaModuliJLogTerm_le_selectedPaperTerm
    (D : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.InitialThetaData AG TG)
    (v : iutInitialThetaOddModSupportFinset D) :
    let R := iutInitialThetaOddSupportTorsionQRootSource D
    let s := (iutInitialThetaModuliSelectedSourceEquiv D v).1
    Real.log ‖NumberField.FinitePlace.embedding v.1.maximalIdeal
      (⟨D.E.j, IntermediateField.mem_adjoin_simple_self ℚ D.E.j⟩ :
        ↥(JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.fieldOfModuli D.F D.E))‖ /
      (2 * (D.prime.ℓ : ℝ) *
        (Module.finrank ℚ ↥(JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.fieldOfModuli D.F D.E) : ℝ)) ≤
      ((Arithmetic.ramIdx s.1 : ℝ) * Arithmetic.ordp s.1 (R.qroot s)) *
        Real.log (Arithmetic.residueChar s.1)

theorem iutInitialThetaFullBaseQPilotLogSum_le_selectedPaperSum
    (D : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.InitialThetaData AG TG) :
    iutInitialThetaFullBaseQPilotLogSum D ≤ iutInitialThetaSelectedQRootPaperSum D

theorem iutInitialThetaFullBaseQPilotLogSum_le_representativeSum
    (D : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.InitialThetaData AG TG) :
    iutInitialThetaFullBaseQPilotLogSum D ≤
      ∑ w : (iutInitialThetaOddTateSectionTateParameterData D).places,
        iutInitialThetaSectionRepresentativeTerm D w.1
end
end JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1
```

S1-BND-281 公共声明完整 Lean 类型（与生产文件同一精确导入及实例环境）：

```lean
namespace JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1
open JoshiTeich.Prologue
open scoped BigOperators Classical
universe u
noncomputable section
variable {AG : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.AnabelianGeometry.{u}}
variable {TG : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.TemperedGeometry AG}

theorem iutInitialThetaReferenceAbsLogQ_eq_fullCarrierRestriction
    (D : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.InitialThetaData AG TG) :
    JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.QPilotData.absLogQ
        (IutTorsionQRootSource.iutInitialThetaQPilotData D) =
      ∑ b : (iutInitialThetaOddSupportBaseQPilotSource D).badFinset,
        if b.1 ∈ JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.badPlacesOver D.F D.E D.VBad then
          iutInitialThetaBaseQPilotLogTerm D b else 0

theorem iutInitialThetaReferenceAbsLogQ_le_fullBaseSum
    (D : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.InitialThetaData AG TG) :
    JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.QPilotData.absLogQ
        (IutTorsionQRootSource.iutInitialThetaQPilotData D) ≤
      iutInitialThetaFullBaseQPilotLogSum D

theorem iutInitialThetaReferenceAbsLogQ_le_selectedPaperSum
    (D : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.InitialThetaData AG TG) :
    JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.QPilotData.absLogQ
        (IutTorsionQRootSource.iutInitialThetaQPilotData D) ≤
      iutInitialThetaSelectedQRootPaperSum D

theorem Corollary312QPilotData.absLogQ_le_selectedPaperSum_ofCanonicalInitialThetaLink
    {O : Type u} [CommRing O] (p : ℕ)
    [Fact p.Prime] [Fact (¬ IsUnit (p : O))]
    [IsAdicComplete (Ideal.span {(p : O)}) O]
    (D : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.InitialThetaData AG TG)
    {T : Corollary312Data D.F O p}
    (theta : ThetaPilotData D.F) (thetaBase : theta.index)
    (packetHull : PacketHullData theta.index)
    (heightData : Arithmetic.HeightData D.F)
    (ramification : Arithmetic.RamificationProfile D.F)
    (logVolume : ℝ) (processionLength : ℕ)
    (hEll : T.ell = D.prime.ℓ)
    (hLink : T.scene1.data.link =
      AdelicThetaLinkData.ofInitialThetaData D theta thetaBase packetHull
        heightData ramification logVolume processionLength) :
    let hCompat : QNormTateCompatibility T.scene1.data.link := by
      rw [hLink]
      exact iutInitialThetaLink_qNormTateCompatibility D theta thetaBase packetHull
        heightData ramification logVolume processionLength
    let S := IutQPilotSource.ofCanonicalInitialThetaLink D p theta thetaBase
      packetHull heightData ramification logVolume processionLength hEll hLink
    (S.toSceneQPilot p hCompat).absLogQ p ≤ iutInitialThetaSelectedQRootPaperSum D
end
end JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1
```

S1-BND-282 公共声明完整 Lean 类型（与生产文件同一精确导入及实例环境）：

```lean
namespace JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1
open JoshiTeich.Prologue
open scoped BigOperators Classical
universe u
noncomputable section
variable {AG : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.AnabelianGeometry.{u}}
variable {TG : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.TemperedGeometry AG}

def iutInitialThetaRepresentativeCoefficient
    (D : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.InitialThetaData AG TG)
    (v : iutInitialThetaOddModSupportFinset D) : ℝ :=
  let R := iutInitialThetaOddSupportTorsionQRootSource D
  let s := (iutInitialThetaModuliSelectedSourceEquiv D v).1
  let b := R.basePlace s
  Arithmetic.qPilotWeight v.1 /
    ((Arithmetic.relRamIdx b.1 v.1 : ℝ) * Arithmetic.relRamIdx s.1 b.1)

theorem iutInitialThetaModuliJLogTerm_eq_coefficient_mul_selectedPaperTerm
    (D : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.InitialThetaData AG TG)
    (v : iutInitialThetaOddModSupportFinset D) :
    let R := iutInitialThetaOddSupportTorsionQRootSource D
    let s := (iutInitialThetaModuliSelectedSourceEquiv D v).1
    Real.log ‖NumberField.FinitePlace.embedding v.1.maximalIdeal
      (⟨D.E.j, IntermediateField.mem_adjoin_simple_self ℚ D.E.j⟩ :
        ↥(JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.fieldOfModuli D.F D.E))‖ /
      (2 * (D.prime.ℓ : ℝ) *
        (Module.finrank ℚ ↥(JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.fieldOfModuli D.F D.E) : ℝ)) =
      iutInitialThetaRepresentativeCoefficient D v *
        (((Arithmetic.ramIdx s.1 : ℝ) * Arithmetic.ordp s.1 (R.qroot s)) *
          Real.log (Arithmetic.residueChar s.1))

theorem iutInitialThetaRepresentativeCoefficient_mem_Ioc
    (D : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.InitialThetaData AG TG)
    (v : iutInitialThetaOddModSupportFinset D) :
    iutInitialThetaRepresentativeCoefficient D v ∈ Set.Ioc (0 : ℝ) 1

theorem iutInitialThetaFullBaseQPilotLogSum_eq_coefficientWeightedPaperSum
    (D : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.InitialThetaData AG TG) :
    iutInitialThetaFullBaseQPilotLogSum D =
      ∑ v : iutInitialThetaOddModSupportFinset D,
        let R := iutInitialThetaOddSupportTorsionQRootSource D
        let s := (iutInitialThetaModuliSelectedSourceEquiv D v).1
        iutInitialThetaRepresentativeCoefficient D v *
          (((Arithmetic.ramIdx s.1 : ℝ) * Arithmetic.ordp s.1 (R.qroot s)) *
            Real.log (Arithmetic.residueChar s.1))

theorem iutInitialThetaFullBaseQPilotLogSum_eq_paperSum_iff_coefficients_eq_one
    (D : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.InitialThetaData AG TG) :
    iutInitialThetaFullBaseQPilotLogSum D = iutInitialThetaSelectedQRootPaperSum D ↔
      ∀ v : iutInitialThetaOddModSupportFinset D,
        iutInitialThetaRepresentativeCoefficient D v = 1
end
end JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1
```

## S1.283：实际 ramification 排除当前去权等式（2026-10-14 03:20:03 CST）

上一 goal turn S1.282 为 progress，09d7218 已推送；开始工作树干净、main 与
origin/main 同步。继续检验上一轮 coefficients=1 条件能否由 actual D 构造，
而不将它作为自由证书。读 actual root source、VBad qOrder coprimality 和
IutRootClassRamification 的参考推导后，发现 actual root equation 本身给出更
直接的相对 ramification 限制。本轮证明去权等式在当前两种归一化下的严格差额。

新窄叶子 IutInitialThetaRepresentativeRamificationGap.lean 仅精确导入
RepresentativeCoefficients 和 VBadRepresentativeBound。对 actual full torsion
source s，其 contracted b 属于实际 VBad pullback 时，对 actual
`qroot(s)^(2*ℓ)=completionMap(s)(B.tateParameter b)` 应用 Valued.v。canonical
completion map 将 valuation 变为 base valuation 的相对 e 次幂，base valuation
为 exp(-qOrder)。root 非零使其 valuation 的 WithZero.log 是整数；exp 注入性
给出 `(2*ℓ)*log(v(root))=-e*qOrder`。由整数整除转换到 Nat，ℓ∣e*qOrder；actual
VBad 上 qOrder 与ℓ互素，故ℓ∣e。该步骤直接消费已有 actual q-root，不另要
root-class/ramification alignment certificate。

选定 v∈VBad、s=sect(v)、b=contraction(s)，其 coefficient 的分母含 torsion/base
相对 e≥ℓ≥5，另一层 e≥1；moduli qPilotWeight≤1。因此c(v)<1。实际 VBad 非空
给出这样一个 moduli support 处，S1.282 equality iff所有c=1排除full=paper，
已有full≤paper使其严格小于；actual reference absLogQ≤full再给actual<paper。

S1-BND-283 公共声明完整 Lean 类型（与生产文件同一精确导入及实例环境）：

```lean
namespace JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1
open JoshiTeich.Prologue WithZero
open scoped BigOperators Classical Valued
universe u
noncomputable section
variable {AG : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.AnabelianGeometry.{u}}
variable {TG : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.TemperedGeometry AG}

theorem iutInitialThetaEll_dvd_qRootRelativeRamIdx_on_vbad
    (D : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.InitialThetaData AG TG)
    (s : (iutInitialThetaOddSupportTorsionQRootSource D).badFinset)
    (hw : ((iutInitialThetaOddSupportTorsionQRootSource D).basePlace s).1 ∈
      JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.badPlacesOver D.F D.E D.VBad) :
    D.prime.ℓ ∣ Arithmetic.relRamIdx s.1
      ((iutInitialThetaOddSupportTorsionQRootSource D).basePlace s).1

theorem iutInitialThetaRepresentativeCoefficient_lt_one_on_vbad
    (D : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.InitialThetaData AG TG)
    (v : iutInitialThetaOddModSupportFinset D) (hv : v.1 ∈ D.VBad) :
    iutInitialThetaRepresentativeCoefficient D v < 1

theorem iutInitialThetaFullBaseQPilotLogSum_lt_selectedPaperSum
    (D : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.InitialThetaData AG TG) :
    iutInitialThetaFullBaseQPilotLogSum D < iutInitialThetaSelectedQRootPaperSum D

theorem iutInitialThetaReferenceAbsLogQ_lt_selectedPaperSum
    (D : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.InitialThetaData AG TG) :
    JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.QPilotData.absLogQ
        (IutTorsionQRootSource.iutInitialThetaQPilotData D) <
      iutInitialThetaSelectedQRootPaperSum D
end
end JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1
```

```lean
namespace JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1
open JoshiTeich.Prologue
open scoped BigOperators Classical Valued
universe u
noncomputable section
variable {AG : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.AnabelianGeometry.{u}}
variable {TG : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.TemperedGeometry AG}

theorem iutInitialThetaFullQRoot_norm_lt_one
    (D : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.InitialThetaData AG TG)
    (s : (iutInitialThetaOddSupportTorsionQRootSource D).badFinset) :
    ‖(iutInitialThetaOddSupportTorsionQRootSource D).qroot s‖ < 1

def iutInitialThetaQRootIntegralBall
    (D : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.InitialThetaData AG TG)
    (s : (iutInitialThetaOddSupportTorsionQRootSource D).badFinset) :
    Set (s.1.maximalIdeal.adicCompletion ↥D.prime.torsionField) :=
  (fun x => (iutInitialThetaOddSupportTorsionQRootSource D).qroot s * x) ''
    Metric.closedBall 0 1

theorem iutInitialThetaQRootIntegralBall_haar_toReal_eq_norm
    (D : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.InitialThetaData AG TG)
    (s : (iutInitialThetaOddSupportTorsionQRootSource D).badFinset) :
    (LocalCompletion.normalizedHaar s.1 (iutInitialThetaQRootIntegralBall D s)).toReal =
      ‖(iutInitialThetaOddSupportTorsionQRootSource D).qroot s‖

def iutInitialThetaPaperBallVolume
    (D : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.InitialThetaData AG TG)
    (s : (iutInitialThetaOddSupportTorsionQRootSource D).badFinset) : ℝ :=
  (LocalCompletion.normalizedHaar s.1 (iutInitialThetaQRootIntegralBall D s)).toReal ^
    (1 / (Arithmetic.inertDeg s.1 : ℝ))

theorem iutInitialThetaPaperBallVolume_mem_Ioo
    (D : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.InitialThetaData AG TG)
    (s : (iutInitialThetaOddSupportTorsionQRootSource D).badFinset) :
    iutInitialThetaPaperBallVolume D s ∈ Set.Ioo (0 : ℝ) 1

theorem iutInitialThetaPaperBallVolume_negLog_eq_paperTerm
    (D : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.InitialThetaData AG TG)
    (s : (iutInitialThetaOddSupportTorsionQRootSource D).badFinset) :
    -Real.log (iutInitialThetaPaperBallVolume D s) =
      ((Arithmetic.ramIdx s.1 : ℝ) * Arithmetic.ordp s.1
        ((iutInitialThetaOddSupportTorsionQRootSource D).qroot s)) *
          Real.log (Arithmetic.residueChar s.1)

def iutInitialThetaSelectedPaperBallProduct
    (D : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.InitialThetaData AG TG) : ℝ :=
  let S := {s : (iutInitialThetaOddSupportTorsionQRootSource D).badFinset //
    s ∈ iutInitialThetaOddSupportSelectedQRootSourcePlaces D}
  letI : Fintype S := (iutInitialThetaOddSupportSelectedQRootSourcePlaces_finite D).fintype
  ∏ s : S, iutInitialThetaPaperBallVolume D s.1

theorem iutInitialThetaSelectedPaperBallProduct_negLog_eq_paperSum
    (D : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.InitialThetaData AG TG) :
    -Real.log (iutInitialThetaSelectedPaperBallProduct D) =
      iutInitialThetaSelectedQRootPaperSum D

theorem iutInitialThetaSelectedPaperBallProduct_mem_Ioo
    (D : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.InitialThetaData AG TG) :
    iutInitialThetaSelectedPaperBallProduct D ∈ Set.Ioo (0 : ℝ) 1
end
end JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1
```

S1-BND-285 公共声明完整Lean类型（与生产文件同一精确导入及实例环境）：

```lean
namespace JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1
open JoshiTeich.Prologue
open scoped BigOperators Classical Valued
universe u
noncomputable section
variable {AG : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.AnabelianGeometry.{u}}
variable {TG : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.TemperedGeometry AG}

def iutInitialThetaFullQRootLogValue
    (D : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.InitialThetaData AG TG)
    (s : (iutInitialThetaOddSupportTorsionQRootSource D).badFinset) :
    s.1.maximalIdeal.adicCompletion ↥D.prime.torsionField :=
  LocalPadicLog.normalizedLog (Arithmetic.residueChar s.1)
    ((iutInitialThetaOddSupportTorsionQRootSource D).qroot s)

theorem iutInitialThetaFullQRootLogValue_norm_eq
    (D : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.InitialThetaData AG TG)
    (s : (iutInitialThetaOddSupportTorsionQRootSource D).badFinset) :
    ‖iutInitialThetaFullQRootLogValue D s‖ =
      ‖(iutInitialThetaOddSupportTorsionQRootSource D).qroot s‖

theorem iutInitialThetaFullQRootLogValue_mem_logShell
    (D : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.InitialThetaData AG TG)
    (s : (iutInitialThetaOddSupportTorsionQRootSource D).badFinset) :
    iutInitialThetaFullQRootLogValue D s ∈
      LocalPadicLog.logShell (k := s.1.maximalIdeal.adicCompletion ↥D.prime.torsionField)
        (Arithmetic.residueChar s.1)

def iutInitialThetaFullQRootLogBall
    (D : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.InitialThetaData AG TG)
    (s : (iutInitialThetaOddSupportTorsionQRootSource D).badFinset) :
    Set (s.1.maximalIdeal.adicCompletion ↥D.prime.torsionField) :=
  (fun x => iutInitialThetaFullQRootLogValue D s * x) '' Metric.closedBall 0 1

theorem iutInitialThetaFullQRootLogBall_eq_rootBall
    (D : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.InitialThetaData AG TG)
    (s : (iutInitialThetaOddSupportTorsionQRootSource D).badFinset) :
    iutInitialThetaFullQRootLogBall D s = iutInitialThetaQRootIntegralBall D s

theorem iutInitialThetaFullQRootLogBall_subset_logShell
    (D : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.InitialThetaData AG TG)
    (s : (iutInitialThetaOddSupportTorsionQRootSource D).badFinset) :
    iutInitialThetaFullQRootLogBall D s ⊆
      LocalPadicLog.logShell (k := s.1.maximalIdeal.adicCompletion ↥D.prime.torsionField)
        (Arithmetic.residueChar s.1)

theorem iutInitialThetaFullQRootLogBall_haar_toReal_eq_norm
    (D : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.InitialThetaData AG TG)
    (s : (iutInitialThetaOddSupportTorsionQRootSource D).badFinset) :
    (LocalCompletion.normalizedHaar s.1 (iutInitialThetaFullQRootLogBall D s)).toReal =
      ‖(iutInitialThetaOddSupportTorsionQRootSource D).qroot s‖

def iutInitialThetaSelectedPaperLogBallProduct
    (D : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.InitialThetaData AG TG) : ℝ :=
  let S := {s : (iutInitialThetaOddSupportTorsionQRootSource D).badFinset //
    s ∈ iutInitialThetaOddSupportSelectedQRootSourcePlaces D}
  letI : Fintype S := (iutInitialThetaOddSupportSelectedQRootSourcePlaces_finite D).fintype
  ∏ s : S, (LocalCompletion.normalizedHaar s.1.1 (iutInitialThetaFullQRootLogBall D s.1)).toReal ^
    (1 / (Arithmetic.inertDeg s.1.1 : ℝ))

theorem iutInitialThetaSelectedPaperLogBallProduct_eq_rootBallProduct
    (D : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.InitialThetaData AG TG) :
    iutInitialThetaSelectedPaperLogBallProduct D = iutInitialThetaSelectedPaperBallProduct D

theorem iutInitialThetaSelectedPaperLogBallProduct_negLog_eq_paperSum
    (D : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.InitialThetaData AG TG) :
    -Real.log (iutInitialThetaSelectedPaperLogBallProduct D) =
      iutInitialThetaSelectedQRootPaperSum D
end
end JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1
```

S1-BND-286 公共声明完整Lean类型（与生产文件同一精确导入及实例环境）：

```lean
namespace JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1.SelectedThetaTensor
open JoshiTeich.Prologue
open scoped BigOperators Classical
universe u
noncomputable section
variable {AG : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.AnabelianGeometry.{u}}
variable {TG : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.TemperedGeometry AG}

def fiber (D : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.InitialThetaData AG TG) (p : ℕ) :
    Finset (iutInitialThetaOddSupportTorsionQRootSource D).badFinset :=
  Finset.univ.filter (fun s => s ∈ iutInitialThetaOddSupportSelectedQRootSourcePlaces D ∧
    Arithmetic.residueChar s.1 = p)

def enumeration (D : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.InitialThetaData AG TG) (p : ℕ) :
    Fin (Fintype.card ↥(fiber D p)) ≃ ↥(fiber D p) :=
  (Fintype.equivFin ↥(fiber D p)).symm

def sourceAt (D : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.InitialThetaData AG TG) (p : ℕ)
    (i : Fin (Fintype.card ↥(fiber D p))) :
    (iutInitialThetaOddSupportTorsionQRootSource D).badFinset :=
  (enumeration D p i).1

theorem sourceAt_selected_residueChar
    (D : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.InitialThetaData AG TG) (p : ℕ)
    (i : Fin (Fintype.card ↥(fiber D p))) :
    sourceAt D p i ∈ iutInitialThetaOddSupportSelectedQRootSourcePlaces D ∧
      Arithmetic.residueChar (sourceAt D p i).1 = p

def packet (D : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.InitialThetaData AG TG) (p : ℕ) :
    LocalCompletion.Packet ↥D.prime.torsionField p (Fin (Fintype.card ↥(fiber D p))) :=
  ⟨fun i => (sourceAt D p i).1, fun i => (sourceAt_selected_residueChar D p i).2⟩

variable (D : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.InitialThetaData AG TG) (p : ℕ) [Fact p.Prime]

local instance integralModule : Module ℤ_[p] (packet D p).Tensor :=
  Module.compHom (packet D p).Tensor (algebraMap ℤ_[p] ℚ_[p])

def value : (packet D p).Tensor :=
  (packet D p).pure (fun i => iutInitialThetaFullQRootLogValue D (sourceAt D p i))

def region : Submodule ℤ_[p] (packet D p).Tensor :=
  (packet D p).lattice (fun i => iutInitialThetaFullQRootLogBall D (sourceAt D p i))

theorem value_ne_zero : value D p ≠ 0

theorem value_mem_region : value D p ∈ region D p

theorem region_eq_rootBallLattice : region D p =
    (packet D p).lattice (fun i => iutInitialThetaQRootIntegralBall D (sourceAt D p i))

theorem region_le_tensorLogShell : region D p ≤ (packet D p).tensorLogShell

theorem region_isOpen : IsOpen (region D p : Set (packet D p).Tensor)

theorem region_isCompact : IsCompact (region D p : Set (packet D p).Tensor)

def regionIndex : ℕ :=
  (packet D p).scalarIndex (fun i => iutInitialThetaFullQRootLogValue D (sourceAt D p i))

theorem regionIndex_pos : 0 < regionIndex D p

theorem region_normalizedHaar_eq_inv_index :
    (packet D p).normalizedHaar (region D p) = (regionIndex D p : ENNReal)⁻¹

theorem region_normalizedHaar_pos_le_one :
    0 < (packet D p).normalizedHaar (region D p) ∧
      (packet D p).normalizedHaar (region D p) ≤ 1
end
end JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1.SelectedThetaTensor
```

S1-BND-287 公共声明完整Lean类型（PadicHaarTransvection；同生产文件精确导入/实例）：

```lean
namespace JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1.PadicHaar
open JoshiTeich.Prologue MeasureTheory Matrix Set
open scoped Classical ENNReal
noncomputable section
variable {p : ℕ} [Fact p.Prime]
variable {ι : Type*} [Fintype ι] [DecidableEq ι]
variable {E : Type*} [NormedAddCommGroup E] [NormedSpace ℚ_[p] E]
  [FiniteDimensional ℚ_[p] E] [MeasurableSpace E] [BorelSpace E]

theorem productHaar_measurePreserving_transvection
    (t : TransvectionStruct ι ℚ_[p]) :
    MeasurePreserving (toLin' t.toMatrix)
      (Measure.pi (fun _ : ι => (Measure.addHaar : Measure ℚ_[p])))
      (Measure.pi (fun _ : ι => (Measure.addHaar : Measure ℚ_[p])))

theorem haar_measurePreserving_transvection
    (μ : Measure (ι → ℚ_[p])) [μ.IsAddHaarMeasure]
    (t : TransvectionStruct ι ℚ_[p]) :
    MeasurePreserving (toLin' t.toMatrix) μ μ

def basisTransvection (b : Module.Basis ι ℚ_[p] E) (t : TransvectionStruct ι ℚ_[p]) :
    E ≃L[ℚ_[p]] E :=
  (b.equivFunL.trans
    (Matrix.toLin'OfInv t.inv_mul t.mul_inv).toContinuousLinearEquiv).trans b.equivFunL.symm

theorem haar_measurePreserving_basisTransvection
    (μ : Measure E) [μ.IsAddHaarMeasure]
    (b : Module.Basis ι ℚ_[p] E) (t : TransvectionStruct ι ℚ_[p]) :
    MeasurePreserving (basisTransvection b t) μ μ
end
end JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1.PadicHaar
```

S1-BND-287 公共声明完整Lean类型（IutInitialThetaSelectedTensorShear；同生产文件精确导入/实例）：

```lean
namespace JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1.SelectedThetaTensor
open JoshiTeich.Prologue MeasureTheory Matrix Set
open scoped Classical ENNReal
noncomputable section
universe u
variable {AG : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.AnabelianGeometry.{u}}
variable {TG : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.TemperedGeometry AG}
variable (D : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.InitialThetaData AG TG) (p : ℕ) [Fact p.Prime]
local instance indexFintype : Fintype (packet D p).BasisIndex := by infer_instance
local instance integralModule : Module ℤ_[p] (packet D p).Tensor :=
  Module.compHom (packet D p).Tensor (algebraMap ℤ_[p] ℚ_[p])

def shear (t : Matrix.TransvectionStruct (packet D p).BasisIndex ℚ_[p]) :
    (packet D p).Tensor ≃L[ℚ_[p]] (packet D p).Tensor :=
  PadicHaar.basisTransvection (packet D p).tensorBasis t

theorem shear_measurePreserving
    (t : Matrix.TransvectionStruct (packet D p).BasisIndex ℚ_[p]) :
    MeasurePreserving (shear D p t) (packet D p).normalizedHaar
      (packet D p).normalizedHaar

theorem region_haar_shear_image
    (t : Matrix.TransvectionStruct (packet D p).BasisIndex ℚ_[p]) :
    (packet D p).normalizedHaar ((shear D p t) '' (region D p : Set (packet D p).Tensor)) =
      (packet D p).normalizedHaar (region D p)

theorem region_haar_shear_image_eq_inv_index
    (t : Matrix.TransvectionStruct (packet D p).BasisIndex ℚ_[p]) :
    (packet D p).normalizedHaar ((shear D p t) '' (region D p : Set (packet D p).Tensor)) =
      (regionIndex D p : ENNReal)⁻¹
end
end JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1.SelectedThetaTensor
```

S1-BND-288 公共声明完整Lean类型（PadicHaarScalarNorm；同生产文件导入/实例）：

```lean
namespace JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1.PadicHaar
open JoshiTeich.Prologue MeasureTheory Matrix Set
open scoped BigOperators Classical ENNReal
noncomputable section
variable {p : ℕ} [Fact p.Prime]


theorem distribHaarChar_eq_norm (a : ℚ_[p]ˣ) :
    (distribHaarChar ℚ_[p] a : ℝ) = ‖(a : ℚ_[p])‖

theorem measure_image_mul_eq_norm (μ : Measure ℚ_[p]) [μ.IsAddHaarMeasure]
    (a : ℚ_[p]) (ha : a ≠ 0) (s : Set ℚ_[p]) :
    μ ((fun x => a * x) '' s) = ENNReal.ofReal ‖a‖ * μ s

theorem measure_preimage_mul_eq_inv_norm (μ : Measure ℚ_[p]) [μ.IsAddHaarMeasure]
    (a : ℚ_[p]) (ha : a ≠ 0) (s : Set ℚ_[p]) :
    μ ((fun x => a * x) ⁻¹' s) = ENNReal.ofReal ‖a⁻¹‖ * μ s
end
end JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1.PadicHaar
```

S1-BND-288 公共声明完整Lean类型（PadicHaarDeterminant；同生产文件导入/实例）：

```lean
namespace JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1.PadicHaar
open JoshiTeich.Prologue MeasureTheory Matrix Set
open scoped BigOperators Classical ENNReal
noncomputable section
variable {p : ℕ} [Fact p.Prime]
variable {ι : Type*} [Fintype ι] [DecidableEq ι]
variable {E : Type*} [NormedAddCommGroup E] [NormedSpace ℚ_[p] E]
  [FiniteDimensional ℚ_[p] E] [MeasurableSpace E] [BorelSpace E]


theorem map_matrix_productHaar_eq_norm_det_inv
    (M : Matrix ι ι ℚ_[p]) (hM : M.det ≠ 0) :
    Measure.map (toLin' M) (Measure.pi (fun _ : ι => (Measure.addHaar : Measure ℚ_[p]))) =
      ENNReal.ofReal ‖M.det⁻¹‖ •
        Measure.pi (fun _ : ι => (Measure.addHaar : Measure ℚ_[p]))

theorem map_matrix_haar_eq_norm_det_inv (μ : Measure (ι → ℚ_[p])) [μ.IsAddHaarMeasure]
    (M : Matrix ι ι ℚ_[p]) (hM : M.det ≠ 0) :
    Measure.map (toLin' M) μ = ENNReal.ofReal ‖M.det⁻¹‖ • μ

theorem map_linearEquiv_haar_eq_norm_det_inv (μ : Measure E) [μ.IsAddHaarMeasure]
    (f : E ≃L[ℚ_[p]] E) :
    Measure.map f μ = ENNReal.ofReal ‖(LinearMap.det f.toLinearEquiv.toLinearMap)⁻¹‖ • μ

theorem measure_image_linearEquiv_eq_norm_det (μ : Measure E) [μ.IsAddHaarMeasure]
    (f : E ≃L[ℚ_[p]] E) (s : Set E) :
    μ (f '' s) = ENNReal.ofReal ‖LinearMap.det f.toLinearEquiv.toLinearMap‖ * μ s
end
end JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1.PadicHaar
```

S1-BND-288 公共声明完整Lean类型（IutInitialThetaSelectedTensorDetHaar；同生产文件导入/实例）：

```lean
namespace JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1.SelectedThetaTensor
open JoshiTeich.Prologue MeasureTheory Matrix Set
open scoped BigOperators Classical ENNReal
noncomputable section
universe u
noncomputable section
variable {AG : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.AnabelianGeometry.{u}}
variable {TG : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.TemperedGeometry AG}
variable (D : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.InitialThetaData AG TG) (p : ℕ) [Fact p.Prime]

local instance detIndexFintype : Fintype (packet D p).BasisIndex := by infer_instance
local instance detIntegralModule : Module ℤ_[p] (packet D p).Tensor :=
  Module.compHom (packet D p).Tensor (algebraMap ℤ_[p] ℚ_[p])
local instance detTensorFinite : Module.Finite ℚ_[p] (packet D p).Tensor :=
  Module.Finite.of_basis (packet D p).tensorBasis
local instance detFactorAlgebra (i : Fin (Fintype.card ↥(fiber D p))) :
    Algebra ℚ_[p] ((packet D p).Factor i) :=
  LocalCompletion.padicAlgebra ((packet D p).residueChar_eq i)
local instance detFactorFinite (i : Fin (Fintype.card ↥(fiber D p))) :
    Module.Finite ℚ_[p] ((packet D p).Factor i) :=
  LocalCompletion.finite_padicAlgebra ((packet D p).residueChar_eq i)


def logScale : (packet D p).Tensor ≃L[ℚ_[p]] (packet D p).Tensor :=
  (packet D p).continuousScale (fun i => iutInitialThetaFullQRootLogValue D (sourceAt D p i))
    (logScalar_nonzero D p)

def logScaleMatrix : Matrix (packet D p).BasisIndex (packet D p).BasisIndex ℚ_[p] :=
  (packet D p).scaleMatrix (fun i => iutInitialThetaFullQRootLogValue D (sourceAt D p i))
    (logScalar_nonzero D p)

def logFactorNorm (i : Fin (Fintype.card ↥(fiber D p))) : ℚ_[p] :=
  @Algebra.norm ℚ_[p] ((packet D p).Factor i) _ _
    (LocalCompletion.padicAlgebra ((packet D p).residueChar_eq i))
    (iutInitialThetaFullQRootLogValue D (sourceAt D p i))

theorem region_eq_logScale_image :
    (region D p : Set (packet D p).Tensor) =
      (logScale D p) '' ((packet D p).unitLattice : Set (packet D p).Tensor)

theorem region_normalizedHaar_eq_det_norm :
    (packet D p).normalizedHaar (region D p) = ENNReal.ofReal ‖(logScaleMatrix D p).det‖

theorem region_normalizedHaar_eq_factorNorm_product :
    (packet D p).normalizedHaar (region D p) =
      ENNReal.ofReal (∏ i, ‖logFactorNorm D p i‖ ^
        FiniteTensorMatrix.otherDimension
          (fun j => Module.finrank ℚ_[p] ((packet D p).Factor j)) i)

theorem regionIndex_inv_eq_det_norm :
    (regionIndex D p : ENNReal)⁻¹ = ENNReal.ofReal ‖(logScaleMatrix D p).det‖
end
end JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1.SelectedThetaTensor
```

```lean
namespace JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1.LocalCompletion.Packet
open MeasureTheory
open scoped BigOperators Classical
universe u
noncomputable section
variable {K : Type u} [Field K] [NumberField K] {p : ℕ} [Fact p.Prime]
variable {ι : Type} [Fintype ι] (P : Packet K p ι)

local instance detFactorAlgebra (i : ι) : Algebra ℚ_[p] (P.Factor i) :=
  padicAlgebra (P.residueChar_eq i)
local instance detFactorFinite (i : ι) : Module.Finite ℚ_[p] (P.Factor i) :=
  finite_padicAlgebra (P.residueChar_eq i)
local instance detBasisIndexFintype : Fintype P.BasisIndex := by infer_instance
local instance detTensorFinite : Module.Finite ℚ_[p] P.Tensor :=
  Module.Finite.of_basis P.tensorBasis
local instance detIntegralModule : Module ℤ_[p] P.Tensor :=
  Module.compHom P.Tensor (algebraMap ℤ_[p] ℚ_[p])


theorem normalizedHaar_scalarLattice_eq_det_norm
    (z : ∀ i, P.Factor i) (hz : ∀ i, z i ≠ 0) :
    P.normalizedHaar (P.scalarLattice z) = ENNReal.ofReal ‖(P.scaleMatrix z hz).det‖

theorem normalizedHaar_scalarLattice_eq_factorNorm_product
    (z : ∀ i, P.Factor i) (hz : ∀ i, z i ≠ 0) :
    P.normalizedHaar (P.scalarLattice z) =
      ENNReal.ofReal (∏ i, ‖Algebra.norm ℚ_[p] (z i)‖ ^
        FiniteTensorMatrix.otherDimension (fun j => Module.finrank ℚ_[p] (P.Factor j)) i)
end
end JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1.LocalCompletion.Packet
```

```lean
namespace JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1.LocalCompletion.Packet
open JoshiTeich.Prologue MeasureTheory
open scoped BigOperators Classical
universe u
noncomputable section
variable {K : Type u} [Field K] [NumberField K] {p : ℕ} [Fact p.Prime]
variable {ι : Type} [Fintype ι] (P : Packet K p ι)

local instance detFactorAlgebra (i : ι) : Algebra ℚ_[p] (P.Factor i) :=
  padicAlgebra (P.residueChar_eq i)
local instance detFactorFinite (i : ι) : Module.Finite ℚ_[p] (P.Factor i) :=
  finite_padicAlgebra (P.residueChar_eq i)
local instance detBasisIndexFintype : Fintype P.BasisIndex := by infer_instance
local instance detTensorFinite : Module.Finite ℚ_[p] P.Tensor :=
  Module.Finite.of_basis P.tensorBasis
local instance detIntegralModule : Module ℤ_[p] P.Tensor :=
  Module.compHom P.Tensor (algebraMap ℤ_[p] ℚ_[p])


def scaleDet (z : ∀ i, P.Factor i) (hz : ∀ i, z i ≠ 0) : ℚ_[p] :=
  (P.scaleMatrix z hz).det

theorem normalizedHaar_scalarLattice_eq_det_norm
    (z : ∀ i, P.Factor i) (hz : ∀ i, z i ≠ 0) :
    P.normalizedHaar (P.scalarLattice z) = ENNReal.ofReal ‖P.scaleDet z hz‖

theorem normalizedHaar_scalarLattice_eq_factorNorm_product
    (z : ∀ i, P.Factor i) (hz : ∀ i, z i ≠ 0) :
    P.normalizedHaar (P.scalarLattice z) =
      ENNReal.ofReal (∏ i, ‖Algebra.norm ℚ_[p] (z i)‖ ^
        FiniteTensorMatrix.otherDimension (fun j => Module.finrank ℚ_[p] (P.Factor j)) i)
end
end JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1.LocalCompletion.Packet
```

```lean
namespace JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1.SelectedThetaTensor
open JoshiTeich.Prologue MeasureTheory
open scoped BigOperators Classical
universe u
noncomputable section
variable {AG : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.AnabelianGeometry.{u}}
variable {TG : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.TemperedGeometry AG}
variable (D : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.InitialThetaData AG TG) (p : ℕ) [Fact p.Prime]

local instance detIndexFintype : Fintype (packet D p).BasisIndex := by infer_instance
local instance detIntegralModule : Module ℤ_[p] (packet D p).Tensor :=
  Module.compHom (packet D p).Tensor (algebraMap ℤ_[p] ℚ_[p])
local instance detTensorFinite : Module.Finite ℚ_[p] (packet D p).Tensor :=
  Module.Finite.of_basis (packet D p).tensorBasis
local instance detFactorAlgebra (i : Fin (Fintype.card ↥(fiber D p))) :
    Algebra ℚ_[p] ((packet D p).Factor i) :=
  LocalCompletion.padicAlgebra ((packet D p).residueChar_eq i)
local instance detFactorFinite (i : Fin (Fintype.card ↥(fiber D p))) :
    Module.Finite ℚ_[p] ((packet D p).Factor i) :=
  LocalCompletion.finite_padicAlgebra ((packet D p).residueChar_eq i)


def logScale : (packet D p).Tensor ≃L[ℚ_[p]] (packet D p).Tensor :=
  (packet D p).continuousScale (fun i => iutInitialThetaFullQRootLogValue D (sourceAt D p i))
    (logScalar_nonzero D p)

def logScaleDet : ℚ_[p] :=
  (packet D p).scaleDet (fun i => iutInitialThetaFullQRootLogValue D (sourceAt D p i))
    (logScalar_nonzero D p)

def logFactorNorm (i : Fin (Fintype.card ↥(fiber D p))) : ℚ_[p] :=
  @Algebra.norm ℚ_[p] ((packet D p).Factor i) _ _
    (LocalCompletion.padicAlgebra ((packet D p).residueChar_eq i))
    (iutInitialThetaFullQRootLogValue D (sourceAt D p i))

theorem region_eq_logScale_image :
    (region D p : Set (packet D p).Tensor) =
      (logScale D p) '' ((packet D p).unitLattice : Set (packet D p).Tensor)

theorem region_normalizedHaar_eq_det_norm :
    (packet D p).normalizedHaar (region D p) = ENNReal.ofReal ‖logScaleDet D p‖

theorem region_normalizedHaar_eq_factorNorm_product :
    (packet D p).normalizedHaar (region D p) =
      ENNReal.ofReal (∏ i, ‖logFactorNorm D p i‖ ^
        FiniteTensorMatrix.otherDimension
          (fun j => Module.finrank ℚ_[p] ((packet D p).Factor j)) i)

theorem regionIndex_inv_eq_det_norm :
    (regionIndex D p : ENNReal)⁻¹ = ENNReal.ofReal ‖logScaleDet D p‖
end
end JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1.SelectedThetaTensor
```

```lean
namespace JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1.LocalCompletion
open JoshiTeich.Prologue NumberField MeasureTheory
open scoped BigOperators Classical Valued
universe u
noncomputable section
variable {K : Type u} [Field K] [NumberField K] {p : ℕ} [Fact p.Prime]
variable (w : FinitePlace K) (hw : JoshiTeich.Prologue.Arithmetic.residueChar w = p)

local instance normFactorAlgebra : Algebra ℚ_[p] (completionAt K w) := padicAlgebra hw
local instance normFactorFinite : Module.Finite ℚ_[p] (completionAt K w) := finite_padicAlgebra hw
local instance normContinuousSMul : ContinuousSMul ℚ_[p] (completionAt K w) :=
  continuousSMul_of_algebraMap ℚ_[p] _ (continuous_padicEmb hw)
local instance normModuleTopology : IsModuleTopology ℚ_[p] (completionAt K w) :=
  isModuleTopologyOfFiniteDimensional


theorem norm_algebraNorm_eq_completionNorm_of_contracting
    (x : completionAt K w) (hx : x ≠ 0) (hx1 : ‖x‖ < 1) :
    ‖Algebra.norm ℚ_[p] x‖ = ‖x‖
end
end JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1.LocalCompletion
```

S1-BND-290 public 完整类型（LocalCompletionDegree，同生产文件精确导入）：

```lean
namespace JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1.LocalCompletion

open NumberField IsDedekindDomain WithZero
open JoshiTeich.Prologue.Arithmetic
open scoped Valued

universe u
noncomputable section
variable {K : Type u} [Field K] [NumberField K]

theorem intValuation_residueChar (w : FinitePlace K) :
    w.maximalIdeal.intValuation (residueChar w : 𝓞 K) = exp (-(ramIdx w : ℤ))

theorem valued_residueChar (w : FinitePlace K) :
    Valued.v (algebraMap K (completionAt K w) (residueChar w : K)) =
      exp (-(ramIdx w : ℤ))

theorem norm_residueChar_eq_inv_pow_localDeg (w : FinitePlace K) :
    ‖algebraMap K (completionAt K w) (residueChar w : K)‖ =
      (residueChar w : ℝ)⁻¹ ^ localDeg w

variable {p : ℕ} [Fact p.Prime]

theorem finrank_padic_eq_localDeg (w : FinitePlace K) (hw : residueChar w = p) :
    letI := padicAlgebra hw
    Module.finrank ℚ_[p] (completionAt K w) = localDeg w

end
end JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1.LocalCompletion
```

S1-BND-290 public 完整类型（LocalCompletionTensorArithmeticVolume，同生产文件精确导入）：

```lean
namespace JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1.LocalCompletion.Packet

open scoped BigOperators Classical
universe u
noncomputable section
variable {K : Type u} [Field K] [NumberField K] {p : ℕ} [Fact p.Prime]
variable {ι : Type} [Fintype ι] (P : Packet K p ι)
local instance arithmeticFactorAlgebra (i : ι) : Algebra ℚ_[p] (P.Factor i) :=
  padicAlgebra (P.residueChar_eq i)
local instance arithmeticIntegralModule : Module ℤ_[p] P.Tensor :=
  Module.compHom P.Tensor (algebraMap ℤ_[p] ℚ_[p])

theorem factor_finrank_eq_localDeg (i : ι) :
    Module.finrank ℚ_[p] (P.Factor i) = JoshiTeich.Prologue.Arithmetic.localDeg (P.place i)

theorem normalizedHaar_scalarLattice_eq_localDeg_product
    (z : ∀ i, P.Factor i) (hz : ∀ i, z i ≠ 0) (hz1 : ∀ i, ‖z i‖ < 1) :
    P.normalizedHaar (P.scalarLattice z) =
      ENNReal.ofReal (∏ i, ‖z i‖ ^
        FiniteTensorMatrix.otherDimension
          (fun j => JoshiTeich.Prologue.Arithmetic.localDeg (P.place j)) i)

end
end JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1.LocalCompletion.Packet
```

S1-BND-290 public 完整类型（IutInitialThetaSelectedTensorArithmeticVolume，同生产文件精确导入）：

```lean
namespace JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1.SelectedThetaTensor
open JoshiTeich.Prologue
open scoped BigOperators Classical
universe u
noncomputable section
variable {AG : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.AnabelianGeometry.{u}}
variable {TG : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.TemperedGeometry AG}
variable (D : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.InitialThetaData AG TG) (p : ℕ) [Fact p.Prime]
local instance arithmeticIntegralModule : Module ℤ_[p] (packet D p).Tensor :=
  Module.compHom (packet D p).Tensor (algebraMap ℤ_[p] ℚ_[p])

theorem region_normalizedHaar_eq_localDeg_product :
    (packet D p).normalizedHaar (region D p) =
      ENNReal.ofReal (∏ i, ‖(iutInitialThetaOddSupportTorsionQRootSource D).qroot (sourceAt D p i)‖ ^
        FiniteTensorMatrix.otherDimension
          (fun j => Arithmetic.localDeg ((packet D p).place j)) i)

end
end JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1.SelectedThetaTensor
```

## S1.291：actual tensor 的 degree-normalized Haar volume 与 q-root log 会计

记录时间：2026-10-14 05:21:29 CST。当前行动：复核tensor weighting来源，推导实际tensor

`refer/iut/Iut/Concrete/LocalConstruct/Volume.lean` 18–23、195–204则使用
`log(haar S/haar I)/[Tensor:ℚ_p]`，且I为maximal order。当前本地Haar以实际
tensor unitLattice归一化，尚未证明它=maximal order。因此本单元仅推导本地
actual unitLattice的degree-normalized数值，不直接移植参考componentVol或
把它命名为论文thetaVolume。

uniformizer数值以1/f纠正；本单元actual degreeVolume权重为1/(e*f)，negative
log每项为ordp(root)*log p，比先前paperTerm=e*ordp(root)*log p少因子e。

不能以本单元1/(e*f)自动替代。此区分不更改公开Core契约或整体数学复选框。

S1-BND-291 public 完整类型（LocalCompletionTensorDegreeVolume，同生产文件精确导入）：

```lean
namespace JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1.LocalCompletion.Packet

open JoshiTeich.Prologue
open scoped BigOperators Classical
universe u
noncomputable section
variable {K : Type u} [Field K] [NumberField K] {p : ℕ} [Fact p.Prime]
variable {ι : Type} [Fintype ι] (P : Packet K p ι)
local instance degreeFactorAlgebra (i : ι) : Algebra ℚ_[p] (P.Factor i) :=
  padicAlgebra (P.residueChar_eq i)
local instance degreeFactorFinite (i : ι) : Module.Finite ℚ_[p] (P.Factor i) :=
  finite_padicAlgebra (P.residueChar_eq i)
local instance degreeIntegralModule : Module ℤ_[p] P.Tensor :=
  Module.compHom P.Tensor (algebraMap ℤ_[p] ℚ_[p])

theorem tensor_finrank_eq_localDeg_prod :
    Module.finrank ℚ_[p] P.Tensor = ∏ i, Arithmetic.localDeg (P.place i)

theorem tensor_finrank_pos : 0 < Module.finrank ℚ_[p] P.Tensor

def degreeLogVolume (S : Set P.Tensor) : ℝ :=
  Real.log (P.normalizedHaar S).toReal / Module.finrank ℚ_[p] P.Tensor

def degreeVolume (S : Set P.Tensor) : ℝ :=
  (P.normalizedHaar S).toReal ^ (1 / (Module.finrank ℚ_[p] P.Tensor : ℝ))

theorem degreeVolume_eq_exp_degreeLogVolume (S : Set P.Tensor)
    (hS : 0 < (P.normalizedHaar S).toReal) :
    P.degreeVolume S = Real.exp (P.degreeLogVolume S)

theorem degreeLogVolume_scalarLattice_eq_norm_sum
    (z : ∀ i, P.Factor i) (hz : ∀ i, z i ≠ 0) (hz1 : ∀ i, ‖z i‖ < 1) :
    P.degreeLogVolume (P.scalarLattice z) =
      ∑ i, Real.log ‖z i‖ / (Arithmetic.localDeg (P.place i) : ℝ)

theorem degreeVolume_scalarLattice_eq_norm_product
    (z : ∀ i, P.Factor i) (hz : ∀ i, z i ≠ 0) (hz1 : ∀ i, ‖z i‖ < 1) :
    P.degreeVolume (P.scalarLattice z) =
      ∏ i, ‖z i‖ ^ (1 / (Arithmetic.localDeg (P.place i) : ℝ))

end
end JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1.LocalCompletion.Packet
```

S1-BND-291 public 完整类型（IutInitialThetaSelectedTensorDegreeVolume，同生产文件精确导入）：

```lean
namespace JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1.SelectedThetaTensor
open JoshiTeich.Prologue
open scoped BigOperators Classical
universe u
noncomputable section
variable {AG : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.AnabelianGeometry.{u}}
variable {TG : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.TemperedGeometry AG}
variable (D : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.InitialThetaData AG TG) (p : ℕ) [Fact p.Prime]
local instance degreeIntegralModule : Module ℤ_[p] (packet D p).Tensor :=
  Module.compHom (packet D p).Tensor (algebraMap ℤ_[p] ℚ_[p])

theorem region_degreeLogVolume_eq_rootNorm_sum :
    (packet D p).degreeLogVolume (region D p) =
      ∑ i, Real.log ‖(iutInitialThetaOddSupportTorsionQRootSource D).qroot (sourceAt D p i)‖ /
        (Arithmetic.localDeg (sourceAt D p i).1 : ℝ)

theorem region_degreeVolume_eq_rootNorm_product :
    (packet D p).degreeVolume (region D p) =
      ∏ i, ‖(iutInitialThetaOddSupportTorsionQRootSource D).qroot (sourceAt D p i)‖ ^
        (1 / (Arithmetic.localDeg (sourceAt D p i).1 : ℝ))

theorem region_degreeVolume_negLog_eq_ordp_sum :
    -Real.log ((packet D p).degreeVolume (region D p)) =
      ∑ i, Arithmetic.ordp (sourceAt D p i).1
        ((iutInitialThetaOddSupportTorsionQRootSource D).qroot (sourceAt D p i)) *
          Real.log p

end
end JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1.SelectedThetaTensor
```

S1-BND-292 public 完整类型及definition（LocalCompletionRelativeDegree，同生产文件精确导入）：

```lean
namespace JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1.LocalCompletion

open NumberField
open JoshiTeich.Prologue.Arithmetic
universe u
noncomputable section
variable {K L : Type u} [Field K] [NumberField K] [Field L] [NumberField L] [Algebra K L]
variable {w : FinitePlace K} {v : FinitePlace L}
variable (hvw : Ideal.LiesOver v.maximalIdeal.asIdeal w.maximalIdeal.asIdeal)

def relativeAlgebra : Algebra (completionAt K w) (completionAt L v) :=
  (iutEmbedCompletion hvw).toAlgebra

section Padic
variable {p : ℕ} [Fact p.Prime] (hw : residueChar w = p)

theorem embedCompletion_padicEmb (x : ℚ_[p]) :
    iutEmbedCompletion hvw (padicEmb hw x) =
      padicEmb ((residueChar_eq_of_liesOver hvw).trans hw) x

theorem relativeAlgebra_padicTower :
    letI := padicAlgebra hw
    letI := padicAlgebra ((residueChar_eq_of_liesOver hvw).trans hw)
    letI := relativeAlgebra hvw
    IsScalarTower ℚ_[p] (completionAt K w) (completionAt L v)

end Padic

theorem finite_relativeAlgebra :
    letI := relativeAlgebra hvw
    Module.Finite (completionAt K w) (completionAt L v)

theorem finrank_relativeAlgebra_eq_ramification_inertia :
    letI := relativeAlgebra hvw
    Module.finrank (completionAt K w) (completionAt L v) = relRamIdx v w * relInertDeg v w

theorem inverse_relativeFinrank_eq_localDeg_ratio :
    letI := relativeAlgebra hvw
    1 / (Module.finrank (completionAt K w) (completionAt L v) : ℝ) =
      (localDeg w : ℝ) / localDeg v

theorem inverse_relativeFinrank_mem_Ioc :
    letI := relativeAlgebra hvw
    1 / (Module.finrank (completionAt K w) (completionAt L v) : ℝ) ∈ Set.Ioc (0 : ℝ) 1

end
end JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1.LocalCompletion
```

S1-BND-292 public 完整类型及definition（IutInitialThetaSelectedTensorRelativeWeights，同生产文件精确导入）：

```lean
namespace JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1.SelectedThetaTensor
open JoshiTeich.Prologue
open scoped BigOperators Classical
universe u
noncomputable section
variable {AG : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.AnabelianGeometry.{u}}
variable {TG : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.TemperedGeometry AG}
variable (D : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.InitialThetaData AG TG) (p : ℕ) [Fact p.Prime]
local instance relativeIntegralModule : Module ℤ_[p] (packet D p).Tensor :=
  Module.compHom (packet D p).Tensor (algebraMap ℤ_[p] ℚ_[p])

abbrev moduliAt (i : Fin (Fintype.card ↥(fiber D p))) :
    NumberField.FinitePlace ↥(JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.fieldOfModuli D.F D.E) :=
  iutPlaceUnder (F := ↥(JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.fieldOfModuli D.F D.E)) (sourceAt D p i).1

def relativeDegree (i : Fin (Fintype.card ↥(fiber D p))) : ℕ :=
  letI := LocalCompletion.relativeAlgebra (iutPlaceUnder_liesOver
    (F := ↥(JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.fieldOfModuli D.F D.E)) (sourceAt D p i).1)
  Module.finrank
    (LocalCompletion.completionAt ↥(JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.fieldOfModuli D.F D.E) (moduliAt D p i))
    (LocalCompletion.completionAt ↥D.prime.torsionField (sourceAt D p i).1)

def relativeWeight (i : Fin (Fintype.card ↥(fiber D p))) : ℝ := 1 / (relativeDegree D p i : ℝ)

theorem relativeDegree_eq_ramification_inertia (i : Fin (Fintype.card ↥(fiber D p))) :
    relativeDegree D p i = Arithmetic.relRamIdx (sourceAt D p i).1 (moduliAt D p i) *
      Arithmetic.relInertDeg (sourceAt D p i).1 (moduliAt D p i)

theorem relativeWeight_eq_localDeg_ratio (i : Fin (Fintype.card ↥(fiber D p))) :
    relativeWeight D p i =
      (Arithmetic.localDeg (moduliAt D p i) : ℝ) / Arithmetic.localDeg (sourceAt D p i).1

theorem relativeWeight_mem_Ioc (i : Fin (Fintype.card ↥(fiber D p))) :
    relativeWeight D p i ∈ Set.Ioc (0 : ℝ) 1

def relativeRootVolume : ℝ :=
  ∏ i, ‖(iutInitialThetaOddSupportTorsionQRootSource D).qroot (sourceAt D p i)‖ ^ relativeWeight D p i

theorem relativeRootVolume_eq_localDeg_product :
    relativeRootVolume D p =
      ∏ i, ‖(iutInitialThetaOddSupportTorsionQRootSource D).qroot (sourceAt D p i)‖ ^
        ((Arithmetic.localDeg (moduliAt D p i) : ℝ) / Arithmetic.localDeg (sourceAt D p i).1)

theorem relativeRootVolume_negLog_eq_ordp_sum :
    -Real.log (relativeRootVolume D p) =
      ∑ i, (Arithmetic.localDeg (moduliAt D p i) : ℝ) *
        Arithmetic.ordp (sourceAt D p i).1
          ((iutInitialThetaOddSupportTorsionQRootSource D).qroot (sourceAt D p i)) * Real.log p

theorem relativeRootVolume_eq_degreeVolume_pow_of_common_moduliDegree (d : ℕ)
    (hd : ∀ i : Fin (Fintype.card ↥(fiber D p)), Arithmetic.localDeg (moduliAt D p i) = d) :
    relativeRootVolume D p = (packet D p).degreeVolume (region D p) ^ d

end
end JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1.SelectedThetaTensor
```

S1-BND-293 public 完整类型及definition（LocalCompletionTensorWeightDescent，同生产文件精确导入）：

```lean
namespace JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1.LocalCompletion.Packet

open NumberField JoshiTeich.Prologue
open scoped BigOperators Classical
universe u
noncomputable section
variable {K : Type u} [Field K] [NumberField K] {p : ℕ} [Fact p.Prime]
variable {ι : Type} [Fintype ι] (P : Packet K p ι)
local instance descentFactorAlgebra (i : ι) : Algebra ℚ_[p] (P.Factor i) :=
  padicAlgebra (P.residueChar_eq i)
local instance descentIntegralModule : Module ℤ_[p] P.Tensor :=
  Module.compHom P.Tensor (algebraMap ℤ_[p] ℚ_[p])

def primeAtScalar (i : ι) (j : ι) : P.Factor j :=
  if j = i then algebraMap ℚ_[p] (P.Factor j) (p : ℚ_[p]) else 1

theorem primeAtScalar_ne_zero (i j : ι) : P.primeAtScalar i j ≠ 0

theorem primeAtScalar_lattice_eq (i j : ι) :
    P.scalarLattice (P.primeAtScalar i) = P.scalarLattice (P.primeAtScalar j)

theorem factor_prime_norm_eq_inv_pow_localDeg (i : ι) :
    ‖algebraMap ℚ_[p] (P.Factor i) (p : ℚ_[p])‖ =
      (p : ℝ)⁻¹ ^ Arithmetic.localDeg (P.place i)

def weightedScalarVolume (γ : ι → ℝ) (z : ∀ i, P.Factor i) : ℝ :=
  ∏ i, ‖z i‖ ^ γ i

theorem weightedScalarVolume_primeAt_eq (γ : ι → ℝ) (i : ι) :
    P.weightedScalarVolume γ (P.primeAtScalar i) =
      (p : ℝ)⁻¹ ^ ((Arithmetic.localDeg (P.place i) : ℝ) * γ i)

theorem weightedScalarVolume_primeAt_eq_iff (γ : ι → ℝ) (i j : ι) :
    P.weightedScalarVolume γ (P.primeAtScalar i) = P.weightedScalarVolume γ (P.primeAtScalar j) ↔
      (Arithmetic.localDeg (P.place i) : ℝ) * γ i =
        (Arithmetic.localDeg (P.place j) : ℝ) * γ j

theorem weightedDegree_eq_of_prime_region_volume (γ : ι → ℝ)
    (V : Submodule ℤ_[p] P.Tensor → ℝ)
    (hV : ∀ i, V (P.scalarLattice (P.primeAtScalar i)) =
      P.weightedScalarVolume γ (P.primeAtScalar i)) (i j : ι) :
    (Arithmetic.localDeg (P.place i) : ℝ) * γ i =
      (Arithmetic.localDeg (P.place j) : ℝ) * γ j

end
end JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1.LocalCompletion.Packet
```

S1-BND-293 public 完整类型及definition（IutInitialThetaSelectedTensorWeightDescent，同生产文件精确导入）：

```lean
namespace JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1.SelectedThetaTensor
open JoshiTeich.Prologue
open scoped BigOperators Classical
universe u
noncomputable section
variable {AG : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.AnabelianGeometry.{u}}
variable {TG : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.TemperedGeometry AG}
variable (D : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.InitialThetaData AG TG) (p : ℕ) [Fact p.Prime]
local instance descentIntegralModule : Module ℤ_[p] (packet D p).Tensor :=
  Module.compHom (packet D p).Tensor (algebraMap ℤ_[p] ℚ_[p])

theorem relativeWeightedPrime_eq_iff_moduliDegree_eq
    (i j : Fin (Fintype.card ↥(fiber D p))) :
    (packet D p).weightedScalarVolume (relativeWeight D p) ((packet D p).primeAtScalar i) =
      (packet D p).weightedScalarVolume (relativeWeight D p) ((packet D p).primeAtScalar j) ↔
        Arithmetic.localDeg (moduliAt D p i) = Arithmetic.localDeg (moduliAt D p j)

theorem no_relative_region_volume_of_distinct_moduliDegree
    (i j : Fin (Fintype.card ↥(fiber D p)))
    (hdeg : Arithmetic.localDeg (moduliAt D p i) ≠ Arithmetic.localDeg (moduliAt D p j)) :
    ¬ ∃ V : Submodule ℤ_[p] (packet D p).Tensor → ℝ,
      ∀ k, V ((packet D p).scalarLattice ((packet D p).primeAtScalar k)) =
        (packet D p).weightedScalarVolume (relativeWeight D p) ((packet D p).primeAtScalar k)

end
end JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1.SelectedThetaTensor
```

S1-BND-294 public 完整类型及definition（IutInitialThetaRelativeQRootFactor，同生产文件精确导入）：

```lean
namespace JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1
open JoshiTeich.Prologue
open scoped BigOperators Classical
universe u
noncomputable section
variable {AG : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.AnabelianGeometry.{u}}
variable {TG : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.TemperedGeometry AG}
variable (D : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.InitialThetaData AG TG)

theorem iutInitialThetaModuliSelectedSource_liesOver (v : iutInitialThetaOddModSupportFinset D) :
    Ideal.LiesOver (iutInitialThetaModuliSelectedSourceEquiv D v).1.1.maximalIdeal.asIdeal
      v.1.maximalIdeal.asIdeal

theorem iutInitialThetaRepresentativeCoefficient_mul_ramIdx_eq_localDeg_div_finrank
    (v : iutInitialThetaOddModSupportFinset D) :
    let s := (iutInitialThetaModuliSelectedSourceEquiv D v).1
    iutInitialThetaRepresentativeCoefficient D v * (Arithmetic.ramIdx s.1 : ℝ) =
      (Arithmetic.localDeg v.1 : ℝ) /
        Module.finrank ℚ ↥(JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.fieldOfModuli D.F D.E)

def iutInitialThetaRelativeQRootFactor (v : iutInitialThetaOddModSupportFinset D) : ℝ :=
  let s := (iutInitialThetaModuliSelectedSourceEquiv D v).1
  letI := LocalCompletion.relativeAlgebra (iutInitialThetaModuliSelectedSource_liesOver D v)
  ‖(iutInitialThetaOddSupportTorsionQRootSource D).qroot s‖ ^
    (1 / (Module.finrank
      (LocalCompletion.completionAt ↥(JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.fieldOfModuli D.F D.E) v.1)
      (LocalCompletion.completionAt ↥D.prime.torsionField s.1) : ℝ))

theorem iutInitialThetaRelativeQRootFactor_eq_localDeg_ratio
    (v : iutInitialThetaOddModSupportFinset D) :
    let s := (iutInitialThetaModuliSelectedSourceEquiv D v).1
    iutInitialThetaRelativeQRootFactor D v =
      ‖(iutInitialThetaOddSupportTorsionQRootSource D).qroot s‖ ^
        ((Arithmetic.localDeg v.1 : ℝ) / Arithmetic.localDeg s.1)

theorem iutInitialThetaRelativeQRootFactor_mem_Ioo
    (v : iutInitialThetaOddModSupportFinset D) :
    iutInitialThetaRelativeQRootFactor D v ∈ Set.Ioo (0 : ℝ) 1

theorem iutInitialThetaRelativeQRootFactor_negLog_div_eq_moduliJLogTerm
    (v : iutInitialThetaOddModSupportFinset D) :
    -Real.log (iutInitialThetaRelativeQRootFactor D v) /
        Module.finrank ℚ ↥(JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.fieldOfModuli D.F D.E) =
      Real.log ‖NumberField.FinitePlace.embedding v.1.maximalIdeal
        (⟨D.E.j, IntermediateField.mem_adjoin_simple_self ℚ D.E.j⟩ :
          ↥(JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.fieldOfModuli D.F D.E))‖ /
        (2 * (D.prime.ℓ : ℝ) *
          Module.finrank ℚ ↥(JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.fieldOfModuli D.F D.E))

end
end JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1
```

S1-BND-294 public 完整类型及definition（IutInitialThetaRelativeQRootProduct，同生产文件精确导入）：

```lean
namespace JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1
open JoshiTeich.Prologue
open scoped BigOperators Classical
universe u
noncomputable section
variable {AG : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.AnabelianGeometry.{u}}
variable {TG : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.TemperedGeometry AG}
variable (D : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.InitialThetaData AG TG)

def iutInitialThetaRelativeQRootProduct : ℝ :=
  ∏ v : iutInitialThetaOddModSupportFinset D, iutInitialThetaRelativeQRootFactor D v

def iutInitialThetaNormalizedRelativeQRootProduct : ℝ :=
  iutInitialThetaRelativeQRootProduct D ^
    (1 / (Module.finrank ℚ ↥(JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.fieldOfModuli D.F D.E) : ℝ))

theorem iutInitialThetaRelativeQRootProduct_pos : 0 < iutInitialThetaRelativeQRootProduct D

theorem iutInitialThetaRelativeQRootProduct_negLog_div_eq_fullBaseSum :
    -Real.log (iutInitialThetaRelativeQRootProduct D) /
        Module.finrank ℚ ↥(JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.fieldOfModuli D.F D.E) =
      iutInitialThetaFullBaseQPilotLogSum D

theorem iutInitialThetaNormalizedRelativeQRootProduct_negLog_eq_fullBaseSum :
    -Real.log (iutInitialThetaNormalizedRelativeQRootProduct D) =
      iutInitialThetaFullBaseQPilotLogSum D

end
end JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1
```

S1-BND-294 public 完整类型及definition（IutInitialThetaRelativeQRootReferenceBound，同生产文件精确导入）：

```lean
namespace JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1
open JoshiTeich.Prologue
open scoped BigOperators Classical
universe u
noncomputable section
variable {AG : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.AnabelianGeometry.{u}}
variable {TG : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.TemperedGeometry AG}

theorem iutInitialThetaReferenceAbsLogQ_le_normalizedRelativeQRootProduct_negLog
    (D : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.InitialThetaData AG TG) :
    JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.QPilotData.absLogQ
        (IutTorsionQRootSource.iutInitialThetaQPilotData D) ≤
      -Real.log (iutInitialThetaNormalizedRelativeQRootProduct D)

theorem Corollary312QPilotData.absLogQ_le_normalizedRelativeQRootProduct_negLog_ofCanonicalInitialThetaLink
    {O : Type u} [CommRing O] (p : ℕ)
    [Fact p.Prime] [Fact (¬ IsUnit (p : O))]
    [IsAdicComplete (Ideal.span {(p : O)}) O]
    (D : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.InitialThetaData AG TG)
    {T : Corollary312Data D.F O p}
    (theta : ThetaPilotData D.F) (thetaBase : theta.index)
    (packetHull : PacketHullData theta.index)
    (heightData : Arithmetic.HeightData D.F)
    (ramification : Arithmetic.RamificationProfile D.F)
    (logVolume : ℝ) (processionLength : ℕ)
    (hEll : T.ell = D.prime.ℓ)
    (hLink : T.scene1.data.link =
      AdelicThetaLinkData.ofInitialThetaData D theta thetaBase packetHull
        heightData ramification logVolume processionLength) :
    let hCompat : QNormTateCompatibility T.scene1.data.link := by
      rw [hLink]
      exact iutInitialThetaLink_qNormTateCompatibility D theta thetaBase packetHull
        heightData ramification logVolume processionLength
    let S := IutQPilotSource.ofCanonicalInitialThetaLink D p theta thetaBase
      packetHull heightData ramification logVolume processionLength hEll hLink
    (S.toSceneQPilot p hCompat).absLogQ p ≤
      -Real.log (iutInitialThetaNormalizedRelativeQRootProduct D)

end
end JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1
```

```lean
noncomputable section
variable {AG : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.AnabelianGeometry.{u}}
variable {TG : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.TemperedGeometry AG}
variable (D : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.InitialThetaData AG TG)

def iutInitialThetaVBadModuliFinset :
    Finset (NumberField.FinitePlace ↥(JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.fieldOfModuli D.F D.E)) :=
  (iutInitialThetaOddModSupportFinset D).filter (fun v => v ∈ D.VBad)

theorem mem_iutInitialThetaVBadModuliFinset_iff
    (v : NumberField.FinitePlace ↥(JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.fieldOfModuli D.F D.E)) :
    v ∈ iutInitialThetaVBadModuliFinset D ↔ v ∈ D.VBad

theorem iutInitialThetaReferenceBaseModuliFiber_eq
    (v : NumberField.FinitePlace ↥(JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.fieldOfModuli D.F D.E))
    (hv : v ∈ iutInitialThetaVBadModuliFinset D) :
    (IutTorsionQRootSource.iutInitialThetaQPilotData D).badFinset.filter
        (fun w => iutPlaceUnder (F := ↥(JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.fieldOfModuli D.F D.E)) w = v) =
      iutInitialThetaBaseModuliFiber D v

theorem iutInitialThetaReferenceAbsLogQ_eq_baseJLogSum :
    JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.QPilotData.absLogQ
        (IutTorsionQRootSource.iutInitialThetaQPilotData D) =
      (∑ w : (IutTorsionQRootSource.iutInitialThetaQPilotData D).badFinset,
        Real.log ‖NumberField.FinitePlace.embedding w.1.maximalIdeal D.E.j‖) /
          (2 * (D.prime.ℓ : ℝ) * (Module.finrank ℚ D.F : ℝ))

theorem iutInitialThetaReferenceAbsLogQ_eq_VBadModuliJLogSum :
    JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.QPilotData.absLogQ
        (IutTorsionQRootSource.iutInitialThetaQPilotData D) =
      (∑ v ∈ iutInitialThetaVBadModuliFinset D,
        Real.log ‖NumberField.FinitePlace.embedding v.maximalIdeal
          (⟨D.E.j, IntermediateField.mem_adjoin_simple_self ℚ D.E.j⟩ :
            ↥(JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.fieldOfModuli D.F D.E))‖) /
        (2 * (D.prime.ℓ : ℝ) *
          (Module.finrank ℚ ↥(JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.fieldOfModuli D.F D.E) : ℝ))

end
end JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1
```

S1-BND-295 public 完整类型及definition（IutInitialThetaVBadRelativeQRootProduct，同生产文件精确导入）：

```lean
namespace JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1
open JoshiTeich.Prologue
open scoped BigOperators Classical
universe u
noncomputable section
variable {AG : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.AnabelianGeometry.{u}}
variable {TG : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.TemperedGeometry AG}
variable (D : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.InitialThetaData AG TG)

abbrev iutInitialThetaVBadModuliAt (v : iutInitialThetaVBadModuliFinset D) :
    iutInitialThetaOddModSupportFinset D := ⟨v.1, (Finset.mem_filter.mp v.2).1⟩

def iutInitialThetaVBadRelativeQRootProduct : ℝ :=
  ∏ v : iutInitialThetaVBadModuliFinset D,
    iutInitialThetaRelativeQRootFactor D (iutInitialThetaVBadModuliAt D v)

def iutInitialThetaNormalizedVBadRelativeQRootProduct : ℝ :=
  iutInitialThetaVBadRelativeQRootProduct D ^
    (1 / (Module.finrank ℚ ↥(JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.fieldOfModuli D.F D.E) : ℝ))

theorem iutInitialThetaVBadRelativeQRootProduct_pos :
    0 < iutInitialThetaVBadRelativeQRootProduct D

theorem iutInitialThetaNormalizedVBadRelativeQRootProduct_pos :
    0 < iutInitialThetaNormalizedVBadRelativeQRootProduct D

theorem iutInitialThetaVBadRelativeQRootProduct_negLog_div_eq_referenceAbsLogQ :
    -Real.log (iutInitialThetaVBadRelativeQRootProduct D) /
        Module.finrank ℚ ↥(JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.fieldOfModuli D.F D.E) =
      JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.QPilotData.absLogQ
        (IutTorsionQRootSource.iutInitialThetaQPilotData D)

theorem iutInitialThetaNormalizedVBadRelativeQRootProduct_negLog_eq_referenceAbsLogQ :
    -Real.log (iutInitialThetaNormalizedVBadRelativeQRootProduct D) =
      JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.QPilotData.absLogQ
        (IutTorsionQRootSource.iutInitialThetaQPilotData D)

theorem iutInitialThetaNormalizedVBadRelativeQRootProduct_eq_exp_neg_referenceAbsLogQ :
    iutInitialThetaNormalizedVBadRelativeQRootProduct D =
      Real.exp (-JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.QPilotData.absLogQ
        (IutTorsionQRootSource.iutInitialThetaQPilotData D))

end
end JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1
```

S1-BND-295 public 完整类型及definition（IutInitialThetaVBadRelativeQRootSceneBridge，同生产文件精确导入）：

```lean
namespace JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1
open JoshiTeich.Prologue
open scoped BigOperators Classical
universe u
noncomputable section
variable {AG : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.AnabelianGeometry.{u}}
variable {TG : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.TemperedGeometry AG}

theorem Corollary312QPilotData.absLogQ_eq_normalizedVBadRelativeQRootProduct_negLog_ofCanonicalInitialThetaLink
    {O : Type u} [CommRing O] (p : ℕ)
    [Fact p.Prime] [Fact (¬ IsUnit (p : O))]
    [IsAdicComplete (Ideal.span {(p : O)}) O]
    (D : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.InitialThetaData AG TG)
    {T : Corollary312Data D.F O p}
    (theta : ThetaPilotData D.F) (thetaBase : theta.index)
    (packetHull : PacketHullData theta.index)
    (heightData : Arithmetic.HeightData D.F)
    (ramification : Arithmetic.RamificationProfile D.F)
    (logVolume : ℝ) (processionLength : ℕ)
    (hEll : T.ell = D.prime.ℓ)
    (hLink : T.scene1.data.link =
      AdelicThetaLinkData.ofInitialThetaData D theta thetaBase packetHull
        heightData ramification logVolume processionLength) :
    let hCompat : QNormTateCompatibility T.scene1.data.link := by
      rw [hLink]
      exact iutInitialThetaLink_qNormTateCompatibility D theta thetaBase packetHull
        heightData ramification logVolume processionLength
    let S := IutQPilotSource.ofCanonicalInitialThetaLink D p theta thetaBase
      packetHull heightData ramification logVolume processionLength hEll hLink
    (S.toSceneQPilot p hCompat).absLogQ p =
      -Real.log (iutInitialThetaNormalizedVBadRelativeQRootProduct D)

end
end JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1
```
