S1-BND-296 public 完整类型及definition（IutInitialThetaRelativeQRootChoice，同生产文件精确导入）：

```lean
namespace JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1
open JoshiTeich.Prologue
open scoped BigOperators Classical
universe u
noncomputable section
variable {AG : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.AnabelianGeometry.{u}}
variable {TG : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.TemperedGeometry AG}
variable (D : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.InitialThetaData AG TG)

theorem iutInitialThetaFullQRoot_pow_norm_inv_eq_torsionJNorm
    (s : (iutInitialThetaOddSupportTorsionQRootSource D).badFinset) :
    (‖(iutInitialThetaOddSupportTorsionQRootSource D).qroot s‖ ^ (2 * D.prime.ℓ))⁻¹ =
      ‖NumberField.FinitePlace.embedding s.1.maximalIdeal
        (algebraMap D.F ↥D.prime.torsionField D.E.j)‖

def iutInitialThetaRelativeQRootFactorAt
    (v : NumberField.FinitePlace ↥(JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.fieldOfModuli D.F D.E))
    (s : (iutInitialThetaOddSupportTorsionQRootSource D).badFinset) : ℝ :=
  ‖(iutInitialThetaOddSupportTorsionQRootSource D).qroot s‖ ^
    ((Arithmetic.localDeg v : ℝ) / Arithmetic.localDeg s.1)

theorem iutInitialThetaRelativeQRootFactorAt_eq_inverse_relativeFinrank
    (v : NumberField.FinitePlace ↥(JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.fieldOfModuli D.F D.E))
    (s : (iutInitialThetaOddSupportTorsionQRootSource D).badFinset)
    (hsv : Ideal.LiesOver s.1.maximalIdeal.asIdeal v.maximalIdeal.asIdeal) :
    letI := LocalCompletion.relativeAlgebra hsv
    iutInitialThetaRelativeQRootFactorAt D v s =
      ‖(iutInitialThetaOddSupportTorsionQRootSource D).qroot s‖ ^
        (1 / (Module.finrank
          (LocalCompletion.completionAt ↥(JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.fieldOfModuli D.F D.E) v)
          (LocalCompletion.completionAt ↥D.prime.torsionField s.1) : ℝ))

theorem iutInitialThetaRelativeQRootFactorAt_mem_Ioo
    (v : NumberField.FinitePlace ↥(JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.fieldOfModuli D.F D.E))
    (s : (iutInitialThetaOddSupportTorsionQRootSource D).badFinset) :
    iutInitialThetaRelativeQRootFactorAt D v s ∈ Set.Ioo (0 : ℝ) 1

theorem iutInitialThetaRelativeQRootFactorAt_negLog_eq_moduliJLog
    (v : NumberField.FinitePlace ↥(JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.fieldOfModuli D.F D.E))
    (s : (iutInitialThetaOddSupportTorsionQRootSource D).badFinset)
    (hsv : Ideal.LiesOver s.1.maximalIdeal.asIdeal v.maximalIdeal.asIdeal) :
    -Real.log (iutInitialThetaRelativeQRootFactorAt D v s) =
      Real.log ‖NumberField.FinitePlace.embedding v.maximalIdeal
        (⟨D.E.j, IntermediateField.mem_adjoin_simple_self ℚ D.E.j⟩ :
          ↥(JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.fieldOfModuli D.F D.E))‖ / (2 * (D.prime.ℓ : ℝ))

theorem iutInitialThetaRelativeQRootFactorAt_eq_of_liesOver
    (v : NumberField.FinitePlace ↥(JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.fieldOfModuli D.F D.E))
    (s t : (iutInitialThetaOddSupportTorsionQRootSource D).badFinset)
    (hsv : Ideal.LiesOver s.1.maximalIdeal.asIdeal v.maximalIdeal.asIdeal)
    (htv : Ideal.LiesOver t.1.maximalIdeal.asIdeal v.maximalIdeal.asIdeal) :
    iutInitialThetaRelativeQRootFactorAt D v s = iutInitialThetaRelativeQRootFactorAt D v t

theorem iutInitialThetaRelativeQRootFactorAt_eq_selectedFactor
    (v : iutInitialThetaOddModSupportFinset D)
    (s : (iutInitialThetaOddSupportTorsionQRootSource D).badFinset)
    (hsv : Ideal.LiesOver s.1.maximalIdeal.asIdeal v.1.maximalIdeal.asIdeal) :
    iutInitialThetaRelativeQRootFactorAt D v.1 s = iutInitialThetaRelativeQRootFactor D v

end
end JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1
```

S1-BND-296 public 完整类型及definition（IutInitialThetaVBadQRootChoice，同生产文件精确导入）：

```lean
namespace JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1
open JoshiTeich.Prologue
open scoped BigOperators Classical
universe u
noncomputable section
variable {AG : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.AnabelianGeometry.{u}}
variable {TG : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.TemperedGeometry AG}
variable (D : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.InitialThetaData AG TG)
variable (ρ : iutInitialThetaVBadModuliFinset D →
  (iutInitialThetaOddSupportTorsionQRootSource D).badFinset)
variable (hρ : ∀ v, Ideal.LiesOver (ρ v).1.maximalIdeal.asIdeal v.1.maximalIdeal.asIdeal)

theorem iutInitialThetaVBadRootSelector_injective : Function.Injective ρ

def iutInitialThetaVBadRelativeQRootProductOf : ℝ :=
  ∏ v : iutInitialThetaVBadModuliFinset D, iutInitialThetaRelativeQRootFactorAt D v.1 (ρ v)

def iutInitialThetaNormalizedVBadRelativeQRootProductOf : ℝ :=
  iutInitialThetaVBadRelativeQRootProductOf D ρ ^
    (1 / (Module.finrank ℚ ↥(JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.fieldOfModuli D.F D.E) : ℝ))

theorem iutInitialThetaVBadRelativeQRootProductOf_eq_canonical :
    iutInitialThetaVBadRelativeQRootProductOf D ρ = iutInitialThetaVBadRelativeQRootProduct D

theorem iutInitialThetaNormalizedVBadRelativeQRootProductOf_eq_canonical :
    iutInitialThetaNormalizedVBadRelativeQRootProductOf D ρ =
      iutInitialThetaNormalizedVBadRelativeQRootProduct D

theorem iutInitialThetaNormalizedVBadRelativeQRootProductOf_negLog_eq_referenceAbsLogQ :
    -Real.log (iutInitialThetaNormalizedVBadRelativeQRootProductOf D ρ) =
      JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.QPilotData.absLogQ (IutTorsionQRootSource.iutInitialThetaQPilotData D)

theorem iutInitialThetaVBadRelativeQRootProductOf_choice_independent
    (τ : iutInitialThetaVBadModuliFinset D → (iutInitialThetaOddSupportTorsionQRootSource D).badFinset)
    (hτ : ∀ v, Ideal.LiesOver (τ v).1.maximalIdeal.asIdeal v.1.maximalIdeal.asIdeal) :
    iutInitialThetaVBadRelativeQRootProductOf D ρ = iutInitialThetaVBadRelativeQRootProductOf D τ

end
end JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1
```

```lean
variable {AG : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.AnabelianGeometry.{u}}
variable {TG : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.TemperedGeometry AG}
variable (D : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.InitialThetaData AG TG)
variable (ρ : iutInitialThetaVBadModuliFinset D →
  (iutInitialThetaOddSupportTorsionQRootSource D).badFinset)
variable (hρ : ∀ v, Ideal.LiesOver (ρ v).1.maximalIdeal.asIdeal v.1.maximalIdeal.asIdeal)

include hρ in
theorem iutInitialThetaVBadRootSelector_injective : Function.Injective ρ

def iutInitialThetaVBadRelativeQRootProductOf : ℝ :=
  ∏ v : iutInitialThetaVBadModuliFinset D, iutInitialThetaRelativeQRootFactorAt D v.1 (ρ v)

def iutInitialThetaNormalizedVBadRelativeQRootProductOf : ℝ :=
  iutInitialThetaVBadRelativeQRootProductOf D ρ ^
    (1 / (Module.finrank ℚ ↥(JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.fieldOfModuli D.F D.E) : ℝ))

include hρ in
theorem iutInitialThetaVBadRelativeQRootProductOf_eq_canonical :
    iutInitialThetaVBadRelativeQRootProductOf D ρ = iutInitialThetaVBadRelativeQRootProduct D

include hρ in
theorem iutInitialThetaNormalizedVBadRelativeQRootProductOf_eq_canonical :
    iutInitialThetaNormalizedVBadRelativeQRootProductOf D ρ =
      iutInitialThetaNormalizedVBadRelativeQRootProduct D

include hρ in
theorem iutInitialThetaNormalizedVBadRelativeQRootProductOf_negLog_eq_referenceAbsLogQ :
    -Real.log (iutInitialThetaNormalizedVBadRelativeQRootProductOf D ρ) =
      JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.QPilotData.absLogQ (IutTorsionQRootSource.iutInitialThetaQPilotData D)

include hρ in
theorem iutInitialThetaVBadRelativeQRootProductOf_choice_independent
    (τ : iutInitialThetaVBadModuliFinset D → (iutInitialThetaOddSupportTorsionQRootSource D).badFinset)
    (hτ : ∀ v, Ideal.LiesOver (τ v).1.maximalIdeal.asIdeal v.1.maximalIdeal.asIdeal) :
    iutInitialThetaVBadRelativeQRootProductOf D ρ = iutInitialThetaVBadRelativeQRootProductOf D τ

end
end JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1
```

## S1.301：selected paper root norm product 与实际 corrected Haar product

| 动态边界字段 | S1-BND-301 |
| --- | --- |
| revision / operation / owner | Prologue-R5 → Prologue-R5；derive；Scene1 |
| declaration / source-id | IutInitialThetaSelectedPaperRootNormProduct.lean的一个definition、三个theorem；JT-SCENE1-INITIAL-THETA-SELECTED-PAPER-ROOT-NORM-PRODUCT |
| target-role / fidelity | 将selected root norm有限乘积识别为actual residue-degree corrected scalar Haar ball product，运输其negative-log=selected paper sum与(0,1)区间性质；不构造论文L'或theta hull |
| source | S1.300 actualPaperRootNorm_eq_rootBallHaar_corrected与S1.284 selected Haar product；独立本地证明，无参考工程代码复制或import |
| model-evidence | 真实D的完整selected source subtype；其有限性来自sourcePlaces_finite，所有因子来自actual qroot。既有actual Haar/norm等式逐项消费；无额外支撑覆盖或数值等式证书 |
| dependency-effect | 单一精确导入低扇出叶子；norm/Haar bridge → actual selected norm product → selected paper ball product及paper sum；SceneBounds四check、四axiom打印、Scene1聚合导入 |

```lean
open JoshiTeich.Prologue
open scoped BigOperators Classical
universe u
noncomputable section
variable {AG : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.AnabelianGeometry.{u}}

def actualSelectedPaperRootNormProduct : ℝ :=
  let S := {s : (iutInitialThetaOddSupportTorsionQRootSource D).badFinset //
    s ∈ iutInitialThetaOddSupportSelectedQRootSourcePlaces D}
  letI : Fintype S := (iutInitialThetaOddSupportSelectedQRootSourcePlaces_finite D).fintype
  ∏ s : S, actualPaperRootNorm D s.1

theorem actualSelectedPaperRootNormProduct_eq_selectedPaperBallProduct :
    actualSelectedPaperRootNormProduct D = iutInitialThetaSelectedPaperBallProduct D

theorem actualSelectedPaperRootNormProduct_mem_Ioo :
    actualSelectedPaperRootNormProduct D ∈ Set.Ioo (0 : ℝ) 1

theorem actualSelectedPaperRootNormProduct_negLog_eq_paperSum :
    -Real.log (actualSelectedPaperRootNormProduct D) =
      iutInitialThetaSelectedQRootPaperSum D

end
end JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1
```

```lean
namespace JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1.RepeatedThetaRoot
open JoshiTeich.Prologue
open scoped BigOperators Classical
universe u
noncomputable section
variable {AG : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.AnabelianGeometry.{u}}
variable {TG : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.TemperedGeometry AG}
variable (D : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.InitialThetaData AG TG)
variable (s : (iutInitialThetaOddSupportTorsionQRootSource D).badFinset) (n : ℕ)

local instance sourcePrime : Fact (Arithmetic.residueChar s.1).Prime :=
  ⟨Arithmetic.residueChar_prime s.1⟩

abbrev packet := LocalCompletion.Packet.repeatedPacket s.1
  (show Arithmetic.residueChar s.1 = Arithmetic.residueChar s.1 from rfl) n

local instance integralModule : Module ℤ_[Arithmetic.residueChar s.1] (packet D s n).Tensor :=
  Module.compHom (packet D s n).Tensor
    (algebraMap ℤ_[Arithmetic.residueChar s.1] ℚ_[Arithmetic.residueChar s.1])

def region : Submodule ℤ_[Arithmetic.residueChar s.1] (packet D s n).Tensor :=
  (packet D s n).scalarLattice (fun _ => iutInitialThetaFullQRootLogValue D s)

def moduliVolume
    (v : NumberField.FinitePlace ↥(JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.fieldOfModuli D.F D.E)) : ℝ :=
  (packet D s n).degreeVolume (region D s n) ^ Arithmetic.localDeg v

theorem region_degreeVolume_eq_rootNorm :
    (packet D s n).degreeVolume (region D s n) =
      ‖(iutInitialThetaOddSupportTorsionQRootSource D).qroot s‖ ^
        ((n : ℝ) / Arithmetic.localDeg s.1)

theorem moduliVolume_eq_relativeFactor_pow
    (v : NumberField.FinitePlace ↥(JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.fieldOfModuli D.F D.E)) :
    moduliVolume D s n v = iutInitialThetaRelativeQRootFactorAt D v s ^ n

theorem moduliVolume_pos
    (v : NumberField.FinitePlace ↥(JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.fieldOfModuli D.F D.E)) :
    0 < moduliVolume D s n v

end
end JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1.RepeatedThetaRoot
```

Public完整类型及definition（IutInitialThetaVBadRepeatedHaarProduct，同生产文件精确导入）：

```lean
namespace JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1
open JoshiTeich.Prologue
open scoped BigOperators Classical
universe u
noncomputable section
variable {AG : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.AnabelianGeometry.{u}}
variable {TG : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.TemperedGeometry AG}
variable (D : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.InitialThetaData AG TG)
variable (ρ : iutInitialThetaVBadModuliFinset D →
  (iutInitialThetaOddSupportTorsionQRootSource D).badFinset)
variable (n : ℕ)

def iutInitialThetaVBadRepeatedHaarProduct : ℝ :=
  (∏ v : iutInitialThetaVBadModuliFinset D, RepeatedThetaRoot.moduliVolume D (ρ v) n v.1) ^
    (1 / (Module.finrank ℚ ↥(JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.fieldOfModuli D.F D.E) : ℝ))

variable (hρ : ∀ v, Ideal.LiesOver (ρ v).1.maximalIdeal.asIdeal v.1.maximalIdeal.asIdeal)

include hρ in
theorem iutInitialThetaVBadRepeatedHaarProduct_eq_normalizedRootProduct_pow :
    iutInitialThetaVBadRepeatedHaarProduct D ρ n =
      iutInitialThetaNormalizedVBadRelativeQRootProduct D ^ n

include hρ in
theorem iutInitialThetaVBadRepeatedHaarProduct_negLog_eq_mul_referenceAbsLogQ :
    -Real.log (iutInitialThetaVBadRepeatedHaarProduct D ρ n) =
      (n : ℝ) * JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.QPilotData.absLogQ
        (IutTorsionQRootSource.iutInitialThetaQPilotData D)

include hρ in
theorem iutInitialThetaVBadRepeatedHaarProduct_choice_independent
    (τ : iutInitialThetaVBadModuliFinset D → (iutInitialThetaOddSupportTorsionQRootSource D).badFinset)
    (hτ : ∀ v, Ideal.LiesOver (τ v).1.maximalIdeal.asIdeal v.1.maximalIdeal.asIdeal) :
    iutInitialThetaVBadRepeatedHaarProduct D ρ n = iutInitialThetaVBadRepeatedHaarProduct D τ n

end
end JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1
```

Public完整类型及definition（LocalCompletionTensorVolumeMonotonicity，同生产文件精确导入）：

```lean
namespace JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1.LocalCompletion.Packet

open MeasureTheory
universe u
noncomputable section
variable {K : Type u} [Field K] [NumberField K] {p : ℕ} [Fact p.Prime]
variable {ι : Type} [Fintype ι] (P : Packet K p ι)
local instance integralModule : Module ℤ_[p] P.Tensor :=
  Module.compHom P.Tensor (algebraMap ℤ_[p] ℚ_[p])

theorem degreeVolume_mono {S T : Set P.Tensor}
    (hST : S ⊆ T) (hT : P.normalizedHaar T ≠ ⊤) :
    P.degreeVolume S ≤ P.degreeVolume T
theorem degreeVolume_unitLattice :
    P.degreeVolume (P.unitLattice : Set P.Tensor) = 1
theorem degreeVolume_le_one_of_subset_unitLattice (S : Set P.Tensor)
    (hS : S ⊆ P.unitLattice) : P.degreeVolume S ≤ 1
end
end JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1.LocalCompletion.Packet
```

Public完整类型及definition（IutInitialThetaVBadRegionVolumeBound，同生产文件精确导入）：

```lean
namespace JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1
open JoshiTeich.Prologue MeasureTheory
open scoped BigOperators Classical
universe u
noncomputable section
variable {AG : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.AnabelianGeometry.{u}}
variable {TG : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.TemperedGeometry AG}
variable (D : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.InitialThetaData AG TG)
variable (ρ : iutInitialThetaVBadModuliFinset D →
  (iutInitialThetaOddSupportTorsionQRootSource D).badFinset)
variable (n : ℕ)
local instance sourcePrime (v : iutInitialThetaVBadModuliFinset D) :
    Fact (Arithmetic.residueChar (ρ v).1).Prime :=
  ⟨Arithmetic.residueChar_prime (ρ v).1⟩
local instance integralModule (v : iutInitialThetaVBadModuliFinset D) :
    Module ℤ_[Arithmetic.residueChar (ρ v).1] (RepeatedThetaRoot.packet D (ρ v) n).Tensor :=
  Module.compHom (RepeatedThetaRoot.packet D (ρ v) n).Tensor
    (algebraMap ℤ_[Arithmetic.residueChar (ρ v).1] ℚ_[Arithmetic.residueChar (ρ v).1])
variable (H : ∀ v : iutInitialThetaVBadModuliFinset D,
  Set (RepeatedThetaRoot.packet D (ρ v) n).Tensor)

def iutInitialThetaVBadRegionVolume : ℝ :=
  (∏ v : iutInitialThetaVBadModuliFinset D,
    (RepeatedThetaRoot.packet D (ρ v) n).degreeVolume (H v) ^ Arithmetic.localDeg v.1) ^
      (1 / (Module.finrank ℚ ↥(JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.fieldOfModuli D.F D.E) : ℝ))

theorem iutInitialThetaVBadRepeatedHaarProduct_le_regionVolume
    (hcontains : ∀ v, (RepeatedThetaRoot.region D (ρ v) n :
      Set (RepeatedThetaRoot.packet D (ρ v) n).Tensor) ⊆ H v)
    (hfinite : ∀ v, (RepeatedThetaRoot.packet D (ρ v) n).normalizedHaar (H v) ≠ ⊤) :
    iutInitialThetaVBadRepeatedHaarProduct D ρ n ≤ iutInitialThetaVBadRegionVolume D ρ n H
theorem iutInitialThetaVBadRegionVolume_le_one
    (hunit : ∀ v, H v ⊆ (RepeatedThetaRoot.packet D (ρ v) n).unitLattice) :
    iutInitialThetaVBadRegionVolume D ρ n H ≤ 1
theorem iutInitialThetaVBadRegionVolume_pos
    (hcontains : ∀ v, (RepeatedThetaRoot.region D (ρ v) n :
      Set (RepeatedThetaRoot.packet D (ρ v) n).Tensor) ⊆ H v)
    (hfinite : ∀ v, (RepeatedThetaRoot.packet D (ρ v) n).normalizedHaar (H v) ≠ ⊤) :
    0 < iutInitialThetaVBadRegionVolume D ρ n H
variable (hρ : ∀ v, Ideal.LiesOver (ρ v).1.maximalIdeal.asIdeal v.1.maximalIdeal.asIdeal)

include hρ in
theorem iutInitialThetaVBadRegionVolume_absLog_le
    (hcontains : ∀ v, (RepeatedThetaRoot.region D (ρ v) n :
      Set (RepeatedThetaRoot.packet D (ρ v) n).Tensor) ⊆ H v)
    (hunit : ∀ v, H v ⊆ (RepeatedThetaRoot.packet D (ρ v) n).unitLattice) :
    |Real.log (iutInitialThetaVBadRegionVolume D ρ n H)| ≤
      (n : ℝ) * JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.QPilotData.absLogQ
        (IutTorsionQRootSource.iutInitialThetaQPilotData D)
include hρ in
theorem iutInitialThetaVBadRegionVolume_negAverageAbsLog_ge_referenceLhs
    (hn : 0 < n)
    (hcontains : ∀ v, (RepeatedThetaRoot.region D (ρ v) n :
      Set (RepeatedThetaRoot.packet D (ρ v) n).Tensor) ⊆ H v)
    (hunit : ∀ v, H v ⊆ (RepeatedThetaRoot.packet D (ρ v) n).unitLattice) :
    -(1 / (n : ℝ)) * |Real.log (iutInitialThetaVBadRegionVolume D ρ n H)| ≥
      -JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.QPilotData.absLogQ
        (IutTorsionQRootSource.iutInitialThetaQPilotData D)
end
end JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1
```

Public完整类型及definition（LocalCompletionTensorIntegralHull，同生产文件精确导入）：

```lean
namespace JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1.LocalCompletion.Packet
open MeasureTheory
universe u
noncomputable section
variable {K : Type u} [Field K] [NumberField K] {p : ℕ} [Fact p.Prime]
variable {ι : Type} [Fintype ι] (P : Packet K p ι)
local instance hullIntegralModule : Module ℤ_[p] P.Tensor :=
  Module.compHom P.Tensor (algebraMap ℤ_[p] ℚ_[p])

def integralHull (U : Set P.Tensor) : Submodule ℤ_[p] P.Tensor :=
  Submodule.span ℤ_[p] U

def scalarGenerators (z : ∀ i, P.Factor i) : Set P.Tensor :=
  {t | ∃ a : ∀ i, P.Factor i, (∀ i, ‖a i‖ ≤ 1) ∧ P.pure (fun i => z i * a i) = t}

theorem integralHull_le_iff (U : Set P.Tensor) (L : Submodule ℤ_[p] P.Tensor) :
    P.integralHull U ≤ L ↔ U ⊆ L := Submodule.span_le

theorem integralHull_iUnion {κ : Type*} (U : κ → Set P.Tensor) :
    P.integralHull (⋃ k, U k) = ⨆ k, P.integralHull (U k)
theorem integralHull_scalarGenerators (z : ∀ i, P.Factor i) :
    P.integralHull (P.scalarGenerators z) = P.scalarLattice z
theorem scalarLattice_le_integralHull (U : Set P.Tensor) (z : ∀ i, P.Factor i)
    (hgen : P.scalarGenerators z ⊆ U) : P.scalarLattice z ≤ P.integralHull U
theorem integralHull_le_unitLattice (U : Set P.Tensor) (hU : U ⊆ P.unitLattice) :
    P.integralHull U ≤ P.unitLattice
theorem integralHull_isOpen (U : Set P.Tensor) (z : ∀ i, P.Factor i)
    (hz : ∀ i, z i ≠ 0) (hgen : P.scalarGenerators z ⊆ U) :
    IsOpen (P.integralHull U : Set P.Tensor)
theorem integralHull_isCompact (U : Set P.Tensor) (z : ∀ i, P.Factor i)
    (hz : ∀ i, z i ≠ 0) (hgen : P.scalarGenerators z ⊆ U) (hU : U ⊆ P.unitLattice) :
    IsCompact (P.integralHull U : Set P.Tensor)
end
end JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1.LocalCompletion.Packet
```

Public完整类型及definition（IutInitialThetaVBadCollatedIntegralHull，同生产文件精确导入）：

```lean
namespace JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1
open JoshiTeich.Prologue
open scoped BigOperators Classical
universe u
noncomputable section
variable {AG : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.AnabelianGeometry.{u}}
variable {TG : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.TemperedGeometry AG}
variable (D : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.InitialThetaData AG TG)
variable (ρ : iutInitialThetaVBadModuliFinset D →
  (iutInitialThetaOddSupportTorsionQRootSource D).badFinset) (n : ℕ)
local instance collatedSourcePrime (v : iutInitialThetaVBadModuliFinset D) :
    Fact (Arithmetic.residueChar (ρ v).1).Prime
local instance collatedIntegralModule (v : iutInitialThetaVBadModuliFinset D) :
    Module ℤ_[Arithmetic.residueChar (ρ v).1] (RepeatedThetaRoot.packet D (ρ v) n).Tensor
variable {κ : Type*}
variable (C : ∀ v : iutInitialThetaVBadModuliFinset D,
  κ → Set (RepeatedThetaRoot.packet D (ρ v) n).Tensor)

def iutInitialThetaVBadCollatedIntegralHull (v : iutInitialThetaVBadModuliFinset D) :
    Submodule ℤ_[Arithmetic.residueChar (ρ v).1] (RepeatedThetaRoot.packet D (ρ v) n).Tensor :=
  (RepeatedThetaRoot.packet D (ρ v) n).integralHull (⋃ k, C v k)
def iutInitialThetaVBadCollatedHullVolume : ℝ :=
  iutInitialThetaVBadRegionVolume D ρ n (fun v => iutInitialThetaVBadCollatedIntegralHull D ρ n C v)

theorem iutInitialThetaVBadCollatedIntegralHull_eq_iSup
    (v : iutInitialThetaVBadModuliFinset D) :
    iutInitialThetaVBadCollatedIntegralHull D ρ n C v =
      ⨆ k, (RepeatedThetaRoot.packet D (ρ v) n).integralHull (C v k)
theorem iutInitialThetaVBadCollatedIntegralHull_le_unitLattice
    (hunit : ∀ v k, C v k ⊆ (RepeatedThetaRoot.packet D (ρ v) n).unitLattice)
    (v : iutInitialThetaVBadModuliFinset D) :
    iutInitialThetaVBadCollatedIntegralHull D ρ n C v ≤
      (RepeatedThetaRoot.packet D (ρ v) n).unitLattice
variable (hgen : ∀ v, (RepeatedThetaRoot.packet D (ρ v) n).scalarGenerators
  (fun _ => iutInitialThetaFullQRootLogValue D (ρ v)) ⊆ ⋃ k, C v k)

include hgen in
theorem iutInitialThetaRepeatedRegion_le_collatedIntegralHull
    (v : iutInitialThetaVBadModuliFinset D) :
    RepeatedThetaRoot.region D (ρ v) n ≤ iutInitialThetaVBadCollatedIntegralHull D ρ n C v
include hgen in
theorem iutInitialThetaVBadCollatedIntegralHull_isCompact
    (hunit : ∀ v k, C v k ⊆ (RepeatedThetaRoot.packet D (ρ v) n).unitLattice)
    (v : iutInitialThetaVBadModuliFinset D) :
    IsCompact (iutInitialThetaVBadCollatedIntegralHull D ρ n C v :
      Set (RepeatedThetaRoot.packet D (ρ v) n).Tensor)
include hgen in
theorem iutInitialThetaVBadRepeatedHaarProduct_le_collatedHullVolume
    (hunit : ∀ v k, C v k ⊆ (RepeatedThetaRoot.packet D (ρ v) n).unitLattice) :
    iutInitialThetaVBadRepeatedHaarProduct D ρ n ≤
      iutInitialThetaVBadCollatedHullVolume D ρ n C
variable (hρ : ∀ v, Ideal.LiesOver (ρ v).1.maximalIdeal.asIdeal v.1.maximalIdeal.asIdeal)

include hgen hρ in
theorem iutInitialThetaVBadCollatedHullVolume_negAverageAbsLog_ge_referenceLhs
    (hn : 0 < n)
    (hunit : ∀ v k, C v k ⊆ (RepeatedThetaRoot.packet D (ρ v) n).unitLattice) :
    -(1 / (n : ℝ)) * |Real.log (iutInitialThetaVBadCollatedHullVolume D ρ n C)| ≥
      -JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.QPilotData.absLogQ
        (IutTorsionQRootSource.iutInitialThetaQPilotData D)
end
end JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1
```

Public完整类型及definition（同生产文件精确导入）：

```lean
namespace JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1
open JoshiTeich.Prologue
open scoped BigOperators Classical
universe u
noncomputable section

theorem negativeAverageAbsLog_nonpos (n : ℕ) (volume : ℝ) :
    -(1 / (n : ℝ)) * |Real.log volume| ≤ 0
theorem corollary312_requires_qLogSum_nonneg
    {K : Type u} [Field K] [NumberField K]
    {O : Type u} [CommRing O] (p : ℕ)
    [Fact p.Prime] [Fact (¬ IsUnit (p : O))]
    [IsAdicComplete (Ideal.span {(p : O)}) O]
    (T : Corollary312Data K O p) (h : Corollary312 p T) :
    0 ≤ Corollary312Data.qLogSum p T
variable {AG : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.AnabelianGeometry.{u}}
variable {TG : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.TemperedGeometry AG}
variable (D : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.InitialThetaData AG TG)

def actualSelectedPaperSignedLogSum : ℝ :=
  let S := {s : (iutInitialThetaOddSupportTorsionQRootSource D).badFinset //
    s ∈ iutInitialThetaOddSupportSelectedQRootSourcePlaces D}
  letI : Fintype S := (iutInitialThetaOddSupportSelectedQRootSourcePlaces_finite D).fintype
  ∑ s : S, Real.log (actualPaperRootNorm D s.1)

theorem actualSelectedPaperSignedLogSum_eq_logProduct :
    actualSelectedPaperSignedLogSum D = Real.log (actualSelectedPaperRootNormProduct D)
theorem actualSelectedPaperSignedLogSum_neg_eq_paperSum :
    -actualSelectedPaperSignedLogSum D = iutInitialThetaSelectedQRootPaperSum D
theorem actualSelectedPaperSignedLogSum_neg_pos :
    0 < -actualSelectedPaperSignedLogSum D
theorem actualSelectedPaper_not_literal_cor312_bound (n : ℕ) (volume : ℝ) :
    ¬ (-(1 / (n : ℝ)) * |Real.log volume| ≥ -actualSelectedPaperSignedLogSum D)
theorem actualSelectedPaper_not_corollary312_of_literal_logTerms
    {K : Type u} [Field K] [NumberField K]
    {O : Type u} [CommRing O] (p : ℕ)
    [Fact p.Prime] [Fact (¬ IsUnit (p : O))]
    [IsAdicComplete (Ideal.span {(p : O)}) O]
    (T : Corollary312Data K O p)
    (hterms : Corollary312Data.qLogSum p T = actualSelectedPaperSignedLogSum D) :
    ¬ Corollary312 p T
end
end JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1
```

```lean
namespace JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1.LocalCompletion.Packet
open JoshiTeich.Prologue
open scoped BigOperators
universe u
noncomputable section
variable {K : Type u} [Field K] [NumberField K] {p : ℕ} [Fact p.Prime]
variable {ι : Type} [Fintype ι] (P : Packet K p ι)
local instance powerIntegralModule : Module ℤ_[p] P.Tensor :=
  Module.compHom P.Tensor (algebraMap ℤ_[p] ℚ_[p])

theorem degreeVolume_scalarLattice_pow_eq_norm_product (d : ℕ)
    (z : ∀ i, P.Factor i) (hz : ∀ i, z i ≠ 0) (hz1 : ∀ i, ‖z i‖ < 1) :
    P.degreeVolume (P.scalarLattice z) ^ d =
      ∏ i, ‖z i‖ ^ ((d : ℝ) / Arithmetic.localDeg (P.place i))
end
end JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1.LocalCompletion.Packet
```

Public完整类型及definition（IutInitialThetaFixedModuliTensorVolume，同生产文件精确导入）：

```lean
namespace JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1.FixedModuliThetaTensor
open JoshiTeich.Prologue
open scoped BigOperators Classical
universe u
noncomputable section
variable {AG : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.AnabelianGeometry.{u}}
variable {TG : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.TemperedGeometry AG}
variable (D : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.InitialThetaData AG TG)
variable (v : iutInitialThetaOddModSupportFinset D)
variable {ι : Type} [Fintype ι]
variable (σ : ι → (iutInitialThetaOddSupportTorsionQRootSource D).badFinset)
variable (hσ : ∀ i, Ideal.LiesOver (σ i).1.maximalIdeal.asIdeal v.1.maximalIdeal.asIdeal)

local instance fixedPrime : Fact (Arithmetic.residueChar v.1).Prime :=
  ⟨Arithmetic.residueChar_prime v.1⟩

abbrev packet : LocalCompletion.Packet ↥D.prime.torsionField (Arithmetic.residueChar v.1) ι :=
  ⟨fun i => (σ i).1, fun i => Arithmetic.residueChar_eq_of_liesOver (hσ i)⟩

local instance fixedIntegralModule :
    Module ℤ_[Arithmetic.residueChar v.1] (packet D v σ hσ).Tensor :=
  Module.compHom (packet D v σ hσ).Tensor
    (algebraMap ℤ_[Arithmetic.residueChar v.1] ℚ_[Arithmetic.residueChar v.1])

def weight (i : ι) : ℝ :=
  letI := LocalCompletion.relativeAlgebra (hσ i)
  1 / (Module.finrank
    (LocalCompletion.completionAt ↥(JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.fieldOfModuli D.F D.E) v.1)
    (LocalCompletion.completionAt ↥D.prime.torsionField (σ i).1) : ℝ)

theorem weight_eq_localDeg_ratio (i : ι) :
    weight D v σ hσ i = (Arithmetic.localDeg v.1 : ℝ) / Arithmetic.localDeg (σ i).1
theorem weight_mem_Ioc (i : ι) : weight D v σ hσ i ∈ Set.Ioc (0 : ℝ) 1
def scalarVolume (z : ∀ i, (packet D v σ hσ).Factor i) : ℝ :=
  (packet D v σ hσ).degreeVolume ((packet D v σ hσ).scalarLattice z) ^ Arithmetic.localDeg v.1

theorem scalarVolume_eq_weightedNorm_product
    (z : ∀ i, (packet D v σ hσ).Factor i)
    (hz : ∀ i, z i ≠ 0) (hz1 : ∀ i, ‖z i‖ < 1) :
    scalarVolume D v σ hσ z = ∏ i, ‖z i‖ ^ weight D v σ hσ i
def region : Submodule ℤ_[Arithmetic.residueChar v.1] (packet D v σ hσ).Tensor :=
  (packet D v σ hσ).scalarLattice (fun i => iutInitialThetaFullQRootLogValue D (σ i))

def volume : ℝ :=
  (packet D v σ hσ).degreeVolume (region D v σ hσ) ^ Arithmetic.localDeg v.1

theorem volume_eq_relativeFactor_pow :
    volume D v σ hσ = iutInitialThetaRelativeQRootFactor D v ^ Fintype.card ι
theorem volume_pos : 0 < volume D v σ hσ
theorem volume_choice_independent
    (τ : ι → (iutInitialThetaOddSupportTorsionQRootSource D).badFinset)
    (hτ : ∀ i, Ideal.LiesOver (τ i).1.maximalIdeal.asIdeal v.1.maximalIdeal.asIdeal) :
    volume D v σ hσ = volume D v τ hτ
end
end JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1.FixedModuliThetaTensor
```

```lean
namespace JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1.LocalCompletion.Packet
open scoped TensorProduct
universe u
noncomputable section
variable {K : Type u} [Field K] [NumberField K] {p : ℕ} [Fact p.Prime]
variable {ι : Type} [Fintype ι] (P : Packet K p ι)
local instance orderFactorAlgebra (i : ι) : Algebra ℚ_[p] (P.Factor i) :=
  padicAlgebra (P.residueChar_eq i)

instance tensorCommRing : CommRing P.Tensor :=
  inferInstanceAs (CommRing (⨂[ℚ_[p]] i, P.Factor i))
instance tensorAlgebra : Algebra ℚ_[p] P.Tensor :=
  inferInstanceAs (Algebra ℚ_[p] (⨂[ℚ_[p]] i, P.Factor i))

instance tensorIntegralAlgebra : Algebra ℤ_[p] P.Tensor where
  smul r x := (r : ℚ_[p]) • x
  algebraMap := (algebraMap ℚ_[p] P.Tensor).comp (algebraMap ℤ_[p] ℚ_[p])
  commutes' r x := Algebra.commutes (r : ℚ_[p]) x
  smul_def' r x := Algebra.smul_def (r : ℚ_[p]) x

theorem pure_mul (a b : ∀ i, P.Factor i) :
    P.pure a * P.pure b = P.pure (fun i => a i * b i)
theorem one_mem_unitLattice : (1 : P.Tensor) ∈ P.unitLattice
theorem mul_mem_unitLattice {x y : P.Tensor} (hx : x ∈ P.unitLattice)
    (hy : y ∈ P.unitLattice) : x * y ∈ P.unitLattice
def unitSubalgebra : Subalgebra ℤ_[p] P.Tensor where
  carrier := P.unitLattice
  mul_mem' := P.mul_mem_unitLattice
  one_mem' := P.one_mem_unitLattice
  add_mem' := P.unitLattice.add_mem
  zero_mem' := P.unitLattice.zero_mem
  algebraMap_mem' r := P.unitLattice.smul_mem r P.one_mem_unitLattice

theorem unitSubalgebra_toSubmodule : P.unitSubalgebra.toSubmodule = P.unitLattice
theorem isIntegral_of_mem_unitLattice {x : P.Tensor} (hx : x ∈ P.unitLattice) :
    IsIntegral ℤ_[p] x
def normalization : Subalgebra ℤ_[p] P.Tensor := integralClosure ℤ_[p] P.Tensor

theorem unitLattice_le_normalization : P.unitLattice ≤ P.normalization.toSubmodule
theorem normalization_isOpen : IsOpen (P.normalization : Set P.Tensor)
theorem normalization_isClosed : IsClosed (P.normalization : Set P.Tensor)
end
end JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1.LocalCompletion.Packet
```

Public完整类型及definition（LocalCompletionTensorResidueDecomposition，同生产文件精确导入）：

```lean
namespace JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1.LocalCompletion
open scoped TensorProduct
universe u
noncomputable section

theorem formallyUnramified_piTensor {R : Type*} [CommRing R] {κ : Type*} [Finite κ]
    (A : κ → Type*) [∀ i, CommRing (A i)] [∀ i, Algebra R (A i)]
    [∀ i, Algebra.FormallyUnramified R (A i)] :
    Algebra.FormallyUnramified R (⨂[R] i, A i)
namespace Packet
variable {K : Type u} [Field K] [NumberField K] {p : ℕ} [Fact p.Prime]
variable {ι : Type} [Fintype ι] (P : Packet K p ι)
local instance residueFactorAlgebra (i : ι) : Algebra ℚ_[p] (P.Factor i) :=
  padicAlgebra (P.residueChar_eq i)
local instance residueFactorFinite (i : ι) : Module.Finite ℚ_[p] (P.Factor i) :=
  finite_padicAlgebra (P.residueChar_eq i)
local instance residueFactorUnramified (i : ι) : Algebra.FormallyUnramified ℚ_[p] (P.Factor i) :=
  Algebra.FormallyUnramified.of_isSeparable ℚ_[p] (P.Factor i)

instance tensorFormallyUnramified : Algebra.FormallyUnramified ℚ_[p] P.Tensor :=
  formallyUnramified_piTensor (fun i => P.Factor i)
instance tensorIsReduced : IsReduced P.Tensor :=
  Algebra.FormallyUnramified.isReduced_of_field ℚ_[p] P.Tensor
instance tensorIsArtinianRing : IsArtinianRing P.Tensor :=
  IsArtinianRing.of_finite ℚ_[p] P.Tensor

def residueDecomposition : P.Tensor ≃ₐ[ℚ_[p]]
    ∀ I : MaximalSpectrum P.Tensor, P.Tensor ⧸ I.asIdeal :=
  (IsArtinianRing.equivPi P.Tensor).restrictScalars ℚ_[p]

theorem residueDecomposition_apply (x : P.Tensor) (I : MaximalSpectrum P.Tensor) :
    P.residueDecomposition x I = Ideal.Quotient.mk I.asIdeal x
theorem eq_of_forall_residue_eq {x y : P.Tensor}
    (h : ∀ I : MaximalSpectrum P.Tensor,
      Ideal.Quotient.mk I.asIdeal x = Ideal.Quotient.mk I.asIdeal y) : x = y
end Packet
end
end JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1.LocalCompletion
```

```lean
namespace JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1.LocalCompletion.Packet
open scoped TensorProduct
universe u
noncomputable section
variable {K : Type u} [Field K] [NumberField K] {p : ℕ} [Fact p.Prime]
variable {ι : Type} [Fintype ι] (P : Packet K p ι)
local instance compactFactorAlgebra (i : ι) : Algebra ℚ_[p] (P.Factor i) :=
  padicAlgebra (P.residueChar_eq i)
local instance compactFactorFinite (i : ι) : Module.Finite ℚ_[p] (P.Factor i) :=
  finite_padicAlgebra (P.residueChar_eq i)

instance normalizationQuotientTower (I : Ideal P.Tensor) :
    IsScalarTower ℤ_[p] ℚ_[p] (P.Tensor ⧸ I) :=
  IsScalarTower.of_algebraMap_eq (R := ℤ_[p]) (S := ℚ_[p]) (A := P.Tensor ⧸ I) fun r => rfl

theorem residueNormalization_module_finite (I : MaximalSpectrum P.Tensor) :
    Module.Finite ℤ_[p] (integralClosure ℤ_[p] (P.Tensor ⧸ I.asIdeal))
theorem normalization_module_finite : Module.Finite ℤ_[p] P.normalization
theorem finite_span_subset_closedBall (s : Set P.Tensor) (hs : s.Finite) :
    ∃ M : ℝ, (Submodule.span ℤ_[p] s : Set P.Tensor) ⊆ Metric.closedBall 0 M
theorem normalization_subset_closedBall :
    ∃ M : ℝ, (P.normalization : Set P.Tensor) ⊆ Metric.closedBall 0 M
theorem normalization_isBounded : Bornology.IsBounded (P.normalization : Set P.Tensor)
theorem normalization_isCompact : IsCompact (P.normalization : Set P.Tensor)
end
end JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1.LocalCompletion.Packet
```

Public完整类型及definition（LocalCompletionTensorNormalizationHaar，同生产文件精确导入）：

```lean
namespace JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1.LocalCompletion.Packet
open MeasureTheory TopologicalSpace
universe u
noncomputable section
variable {K : Type u} [Field K] [NumberField K] {p : ℕ} [Fact p.Prime]
variable {ι : Type} [Fintype ι] (P : Packet K p ι)

def normalizationCompact : PositiveCompacts P.Tensor where
  carrier := P.normalization
  isCompact' := P.normalization_isCompact
  interior_nonempty' := by
    rw [P.normalization_isOpen.interior_eq]
    exact ⟨0, P.normalization.zero_mem⟩

def normalizationHaar : Measure P.Tensor := Measure.addHaarMeasure P.normalizationCompact
instance normalizationHaar_isAddHaarMeasure : P.normalizationHaar.IsAddHaarMeasure :=
  inferInstanceAs (Measure.addHaarMeasure P.normalizationCompact).IsAddHaarMeasure
instance normalizationHaar_regular : P.normalizationHaar.Regular :=
  inferInstanceAs (Measure.addHaarMeasure P.normalizationCompact).Regular

theorem normalizationHaar_normalization : P.normalizationHaar P.normalization = 1
def normalizationMass : ENNReal := P.normalizedHaar P.normalization

theorem one_le_normalizationMass : 1 ≤ P.normalizationMass
theorem normalizationMass_lt_top : P.normalizationMass < ⊤
theorem normalizedHaar_eq_normalizationMass_mul (S : Set P.Tensor) :
    P.normalizedHaar S = P.normalizationMass * P.normalizationHaar S
end
end JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1.LocalCompletion.Packet
```

```lean
namespace JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1.LocalCompletion.Packet
open MeasureTheory
open scoped BigOperators Classical
universe u
noncomputable section
variable {K : Type u} [Field K] [NumberField K] {p : ℕ} [Fact p.Prime]
variable {ι : Type} [Fintype ι] (P : Packet K p ι)
local instance saturationFactorAlgebra (i : ι) : Algebra ℚ_[p] (P.Factor i) :=
  padicAlgebra (P.residueChar_eq i)
local instance saturationFactorFinite (i : ι) : Module.Finite ℚ_[p] (P.Factor i) :=
  finite_padicAlgebra (P.residueChar_eq i)
local instance saturationTensorFinite : Module.Finite ℚ_[p] P.Tensor :=
  Module.Finite.of_basis P.tensorBasis
local instance saturationIntegralModule : Module ℤ_[p] P.Tensor :=
  Module.compHom P.Tensor (algebraMap ℤ_[p] ℚ_[p])
variable (z : ∀ i, P.Factor i) (hz : ∀ i, z i ≠ 0)

theorem scale_eq_mul_pure (x : P.Tensor) : P.scale z hz x = P.pure z * x
def scalarNormalization : Submodule ℤ_[p] P.Tensor :=
  P.normalization.toSubmodule.map (P.scaleIntegral z hz)

theorem scalarNormalization_eq_image :
    (P.scalarNormalization z hz : Set P.Tensor) =
      (P.continuousScale z hz) '' (P.normalization : Set P.Tensor)
theorem scalarLattice_le_scalarNormalization : P.scalarLattice z ≤ P.scalarNormalization z hz
theorem scalarNormalization_mul_mem
    {a x : P.Tensor} (ha : a ∈ P.normalization) (hx : x ∈ P.scalarNormalization z hz) :
    a * x ∈ P.scalarNormalization z hz
theorem scalarNormalization_le_of_mul_closed (L : Submodule ℤ_[p] P.Tensor)
    (hL : ∀ a ∈ P.normalization, ∀ x ∈ L, a * x ∈ L)
    (hregion : P.scalarLattice z ≤ L) : P.scalarNormalization z hz ≤ L
theorem scalarNormalization_le_normalization (hz1 : ∀ i, ‖z i‖ ≤ 1) :
    P.scalarNormalization z hz ≤ P.normalization.toSubmodule
theorem scalarNormalization_isCompact : IsCompact (P.scalarNormalization z hz : Set P.Tensor)
theorem normalizationHaar_scalarNormalization_eq_det_norm :
    P.normalizationHaar (P.scalarNormalization z hz) = ENNReal.ofReal ‖P.scaleDet z hz‖
def normalizationDegreeVolume (S : Set P.Tensor) : ℝ :=
  (P.normalizationHaar S).toReal ^ (1 / (Module.finrank ℚ_[p] P.Tensor : ℝ))

theorem normalizationDegreeVolume_scalarNormalization_eq_degreeVolume :
    P.normalizationDegreeVolume (P.scalarNormalization z hz) = P.degreeVolume (P.scalarLattice z)
end
end JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1.LocalCompletion.Packet
```

Public完整类型及definition（IutInitialThetaFixedModuliNormalizationVolume，同生产文件精确导入）：

```lean
namespace JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1.FixedModuliThetaNormalization
open JoshiTeich.Prologue
open scoped Classical
universe u
noncomputable section
variable {AG : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.AnabelianGeometry.{u}}
variable {TG : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.TemperedGeometry AG}
variable (D : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.InitialThetaData AG TG)
variable (v : iutInitialThetaOddModSupportFinset D)
variable {ι : Type} [Fintype ι]
variable (σ : ι → (iutInitialThetaOddSupportTorsionQRootSource D).badFinset)
variable (hσ : ∀ i, Ideal.LiesOver (σ i).1.maximalIdeal.asIdeal v.1.maximalIdeal.asIdeal)
local instance normalizedPrime : Fact (Arithmetic.residueChar v.1).Prime :=
  ⟨Arithmetic.residueChar_prime v.1⟩
local instance normalizedIntegralModule : Module ℤ_[Arithmetic.residueChar v.1]
    (FixedModuliThetaTensor.packet D v σ hσ).Tensor :=
  Module.compHom (FixedModuliThetaTensor.packet D v σ hσ).Tensor
    (algebraMap ℤ_[Arithmetic.residueChar v.1] ℚ_[Arithmetic.residueChar v.1])

def region : Submodule ℤ_[Arithmetic.residueChar v.1]
    (FixedModuliThetaTensor.packet D v σ hσ).Tensor :=
  (FixedModuliThetaTensor.packet D v σ hσ).scalarNormalization
    (fun i => iutInitialThetaFullQRootLogValue D (σ i)) (logScalar_ne_zero D σ)

def volume : ℝ :=
  (FixedModuliThetaTensor.packet D v σ hσ).normalizationDegreeVolume (region D v σ hσ) ^
    Arithmetic.localDeg v.1

theorem volume_eq_unitVolume : volume D v σ hσ = FixedModuliThetaTensor.volume D v σ hσ
theorem volume_eq_relativeFactor_pow :
    volume D v σ hσ = iutInitialThetaRelativeQRootFactor D v ^ Fintype.card ι
theorem volume_choice_independent
    (τ : ι → (iutInitialThetaOddSupportTorsionQRootSource D).badFinset)
    (hτ : ∀ i, Ideal.LiesOver (τ i).1.maximalIdeal.asIdeal v.1.maximalIdeal.asIdeal) :
    volume D v σ hσ = volume D v τ hτ
end
end JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1.FixedModuliThetaNormalization
```

```lean
namespace JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1.LocalCompletion.Packet
universe u
noncomputable section
variable {K : Type u} [Field K] [NumberField K] {p : ℕ} [Fact p.Prime]
variable {ι : Type} [Fintype ι] (P : Packet K p ι)

theorem isIntegral_iff_residue_integral (x : P.Tensor) :
    IsIntegral ℤ_[p] x ↔ ∀ I : MaximalSpectrum P.Tensor,
      IsIntegral ℤ_[p] (Ideal.Quotient.mk I.asIdeal x)
def normalizationResidueMap : P.normalization →ₐ[ℤ_[p]]
    ∀ I : MaximalSpectrum P.Tensor, integralClosure ℤ_[p] (P.Tensor ⧸ I.asIdeal) where
  toFun x I := ⟨Ideal.Quotient.mk I.asIdeal x,
    IsIntegral.map_of_comp_eq (RingHom.id ℤ_[p]) (Ideal.Quotient.mk I.asIdeal)
      (RingHom.ext fun _ => rfl) x.2⟩
  map_one' := by funext I; exact Subtype.ext (map_one _)
  map_mul' x y := by funext I; exact Subtype.ext (map_mul _ _ _)
  map_zero' := by funext I; exact Subtype.ext (map_zero _)
  map_add' x y := by funext I; exact Subtype.ext (map_add _ _ _)
  commutes' r := by funext I; exact Subtype.ext rfl

theorem normalizationResidueMap_bijective : Function.Bijective P.normalizationResidueMap
def normalizationDecomposition : P.normalization ≃ₐ[ℤ_[p]]
    ∀ I : MaximalSpectrum P.Tensor, integralClosure ℤ_[p] (P.Tensor ⧸ I.asIdeal) :=
  AlgEquiv.ofBijective P.normalizationResidueMap P.normalizationResidueMap_bijective

theorem normalizationDecomposition_apply (x : P.normalization) (I : MaximalSpectrum P.Tensor) :
    (P.normalizationDecomposition x I : P.Tensor ⧸ I.asIdeal) =
      Ideal.Quotient.mk I.asIdeal (x : P.Tensor)
end
end JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1.LocalCompletion.Packet
```

Public完整类型及definition（LocalCompletionTensorScalarResidueRegion，同生产文件精确导入）：

```lean
namespace JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1.LocalCompletion.Packet
universe u
noncomputable section
variable {K : Type u} [Field K] [NumberField K] {p : ℕ} [Fact p.Prime]
variable {ι : Type} [Fintype ι] (P : Packet K p ι)
local instance coordinateIntegralModule : Module ℤ_[p] P.Tensor :=
  Module.compHom P.Tensor (algebraMap ℤ_[p] ℚ_[p])
variable (z : ∀ i, P.Factor i) (hz : ∀ i, z i ≠ 0)

include hz in
theorem pure_isUnit : IsUnit (P.pure z)
def scalarResidueMultiplier (I : MaximalSpectrum P.Tensor) : P.Tensor ⧸ I.asIdeal :=
  Ideal.Quotient.mk I.asIdeal (P.pure z)

include hz in
theorem scalarResidueMultiplier_isUnit (I : MaximalSpectrum P.Tensor) :
    IsUnit (P.scalarResidueMultiplier z I)
include hz in
theorem scalarResidueMultiplier_ne_zero (I : MaximalSpectrum P.Tensor) :
    P.scalarResidueMultiplier z I ≠ 0
theorem scalarResidueMultiplier_isIntegral (hz1 : ∀ i, ‖z i‖ ≤ 1)
    (I : MaximalSpectrum P.Tensor) : IsIntegral ℤ_[p] (P.scalarResidueMultiplier z I)
theorem mem_scalarNormalization_iff_residue_principal (x : P.Tensor) :
    x ∈ P.scalarNormalization z hz ↔ ∀ I : MaximalSpectrum P.Tensor,
      ∃ b : P.Tensor ⧸ I.asIdeal, IsIntegral ℤ_[p] b ∧
        P.scalarResidueMultiplier z I * b = Ideal.Quotient.mk I.asIdeal x
end
end JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1.LocalCompletion.Packet
```

Public完整类型及definition（LocalCompletionTensorResidueNorm，同生产文件精确导入）：

```lean
namespace JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1.LocalCompletion.Packet
open Polynomial
universe u
noncomputable section
variable {K : Type u} [Field K] [NumberField K] {p : ℕ} [Fact p.Prime]
variable {ι : Type} [Fintype ι] (P : Packet K p ι)
local instance normFactorAlgebra (i : ι) : Algebra ℚ_[p] (P.Factor i) :=
  padicAlgebra (P.residueChar_eq i)
local instance normFactorFinite (i : ι) : Module.Finite ℚ_[p] (P.Factor i) :=
  finite_padicAlgebra (P.residueChar_eq i)
local instance normTensorFinite : Module.Finite ℚ_[p] P.Tensor :=
  Module.Finite.of_basis P.tensorBasis

instance residueField (I : MaximalSpectrum P.Tensor) : Field (P.Tensor ⧸ I.asIdeal) :=
  Ideal.Quotient.field I.asIdeal

instance residueFieldFinite (I : MaximalSpectrum P.Tensor) :
    Module.Finite ℚ_[p] (P.Tensor ⧸ I.asIdeal) :=
  Module.Finite.of_surjective (Ideal.Quotient.mkₐ ℚ_[p] I.asIdeal).toLinearMap
    Ideal.Quotient.mk_surjective

def residueNorm (I : MaximalSpectrum P.Tensor) (x : P.Tensor ⧸ I.asIdeal) : ℝ :=
  spectralNorm ℚ_[p] (P.Tensor ⧸ I.asIdeal) x

variable (I : MaximalSpectrum P.Tensor)

theorem residueNorm_nonneg (x : P.Tensor ⧸ I.asIdeal) : 0 ≤ P.residueNorm I x
theorem residueNorm_zero : P.residueNorm I 0 = 0
theorem residueNorm_one : P.residueNorm I 1 = 1
theorem residueNorm_mul (x y : P.Tensor ⧸ I.asIdeal) :
    P.residueNorm I (x * y) = P.residueNorm I x * P.residueNorm I y
theorem residueNorm_eq_zero_iff {x : P.Tensor ⧸ I.asIdeal} :
    P.residueNorm I x = 0 ↔ x = 0
theorem residueNorm_pos {x : P.Tensor ⧸ I.asIdeal} (hx : x ≠ 0) : 0 < P.residueNorm I x
theorem residueNorm_algebraMap (x : ℚ_[p]) :
    P.residueNorm I (algebraMap ℚ_[p] (P.Tensor ⧸ I.asIdeal) x) = ‖x‖
theorem residueNorm_inv (x : P.Tensor ⧸ I.asIdeal) :
    P.residueNorm I x⁻¹ = (P.residueNorm I x)⁻¹
theorem isIntegral_iff_residueNorm_le_one (x : P.Tensor ⧸ I.asIdeal) :
    IsIntegral ℤ_[p] x ↔ P.residueNorm I x ≤ 1
@[instance_reducible] def residueNormedField : NormedField (P.Tensor ⧸ I.asIdeal) :=
  spectralNorm.normedField ℚ_[p] (P.Tensor ⧸ I.asIdeal)

theorem residueNormedField_norm (x : P.Tensor ⧸ I.asIdeal) :
    letI
theorem residueIntegralClosure_eq_closedBall :
    letI
end
end JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1.LocalCompletion.Packet
```

```lean
namespace JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1.LocalCompletion.Packet
universe u
noncomputable section
variable {K : Type u} [Field K] [NumberField K] {p : ℕ} [Fact p.Prime]
variable {ι : Type} [Fintype ι] (P : Packet K p ι)
local instance ballRegionIntegralModule : Module ℤ_[p] P.Tensor :=
  Module.compHom P.Tensor (algebraMap ℤ_[p] ℚ_[p])

theorem mem_normalization_iff_residueNorm_le_one (x : P.Tensor) :
    x ∈ P.normalization ↔ ∀ I : MaximalSpectrum P.Tensor,
      P.residueNorm I (Ideal.Quotient.mk I.asIdeal x) ≤ 1
theorem mem_scalarNormalization_iff_residueNorm_le_multiplier
    (z : ∀ i, P.Factor i) (hz : ∀ i, z i ≠ 0) (x : P.Tensor) :
    x ∈ P.scalarNormalization z hz ↔ ∀ I : MaximalSpectrum P.Tensor,
      P.residueNorm I (Ideal.Quotient.mk I.asIdeal x) ≤
        P.residueNorm I (P.scalarResidueMultiplier z I)
end
end JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1.LocalCompletion.Packet
```

```lean
namespace JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1.LocalCompletion.Packet
open Polynomial
universe u
noncomputable section
variable {K : Type u} [Field K] [NumberField K] {p : ℕ} [Fact p.Prime]
variable {ι : Type} [Fintype ι] (P : Packet K p ι) (I : MaximalSpectrum P.Tensor)

def residueExponent : ℕ := (Module.finrank ℚ_[p] (P.Tensor ⧸ I.asIdeal)).factorial

theorem exists_residueNorm_pow_eq_zpow {y : P.Tensor ⧸ I.asIdeal} (hy : y ≠ 0) :
    ∃ k : ℤ, P.residueNorm I y ^ P.residueExponent I = (p : ℝ) ^ k
theorem exists_least_residueNorm_ge {M : ℝ} (hM : 0 < M) :
    ∃ b : P.Tensor ⧸ I.asIdeal, b ≠ 0 ∧ M ≤ P.residueNorm I b ∧
      ∀ y : P.Tensor ⧸ I.asIdeal, y ≠ 0 → M ≤ P.residueNorm I y →
        P.residueNorm I b ≤ P.residueNorm I y
end
end JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1.LocalCompletion.Packet
```

Public完整类型及definition（LocalCompletionTensorPrincipalRegions，同生产文件精确导入）：

```lean
namespace JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1.LocalCompletion.Packet
open scoped Classical
universe u
noncomputable section
variable {K : Type u} [Field K] [NumberField K] {p : ℕ} [Fact p.Prime]
variable {ι : Type} [Fintype ι] (P : Packet K p ι)

theorem isUnit_iff_residue_ne_zero (a : P.Tensor) :
    IsUnit a ↔ ∀ I : MaximalSpectrum P.Tensor, Ideal.Quotient.mk I.asIdeal a ≠ 0
def principalRegion (a : P.Tensor) : Set P.Tensor :=
  (fun y => a * y) '' (P.normalization : Set P.Tensor)

theorem mem_principalRegion_iff (a : P.Tensor) (ha : IsUnit a) (x : P.Tensor) :
    x ∈ P.principalRegion a ↔ ∀ I : MaximalSpectrum P.Tensor,
      P.residueNorm I (Ideal.Quotient.mk I.asIdeal x) ≤
        P.residueNorm I (Ideal.Quotient.mk I.asIdeal a)
theorem exists_least_principalRegion_of_radii
    (M : MaximalSpectrum P.Tensor → ℝ) (hM : ∀ I, 0 < M I) :
    ∃ a : P.Tensor, IsUnit a ∧
      (∀ I, M I ≤ P.residueNorm I (Ideal.Quotient.mk I.asIdeal a)) ∧
      ∀ b : P.Tensor, IsUnit b →
        (∀ I, M I ≤ P.residueNorm I (Ideal.Quotient.mk I.asIdeal b)) →
        P.principalRegion a ⊆ P.principalRegion b
theorem exists_least_integral_principalHull (U : Set P.Tensor)
    (hU : U ⊆ P.normalization)
    (hcoord : ∀ I : MaximalSpectrum P.Tensor, ∃ x ∈ U, Ideal.Quotient.mk I.asIdeal x ≠ 0) :
    ∃ a : P.Tensor, IsUnit a ∧ a ∈ P.normalization ∧ U ⊆ P.principalRegion a ∧
      ∀ b : P.Tensor, IsUnit b → U ⊆ P.principalRegion b →
        P.principalRegion a ⊆ P.principalRegion b
end
end JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1.LocalCompletion.Packet
```

```lean
namespace JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1.LocalCompletion.Packet
universe u
noncomputable section
variable {K : Type u} [Field K] [NumberField K] {p : ℕ} [Fact p.Prime]
variable {ι : Type} [Fintype ι] (P : Packet K p ι)
local instance boundsFactorAlgebra (i : ι) : Algebra ℚ_[p] (P.Factor i) :=
  padicAlgebra (P.residueChar_eq i)
local instance boundsFactorFinite (i : ι) : Module.Finite ℚ_[p] (P.Factor i) :=
  finite_padicAlgebra (P.residueChar_eq i)

theorem exists_closedBall_subset_normalization :
    ∃ r : ℝ, 0 < r ∧ Metric.closedBall (0 : P.Tensor) r ⊆ P.normalization
theorem exists_uniform_residueNorm_bound {U : Set P.Tensor} (hU : Bornology.IsBounded U) :
    ∃ B : ℝ, ∀ x ∈ U, ∀ I : MaximalSpectrum P.Tensor,
      P.residueNorm I (Ideal.Quotient.mk I.asIdeal x) ≤ B
end
end JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1.LocalCompletion.Packet
```

Public完整类型及definition（LocalCompletionTensorHaarSubspaces，同生产文件精确导入）：

```lean
namespace JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1.LocalCompletion.Packet
open MeasureTheory
open scoped Pointwise Function
universe u
noncomputable section
variable {K : Type u} [Field K] [NumberField K] {p : ℕ} [Fact p.Prime]
variable {ι : Type} [Fintype ι] (P : Packet K p ι)
local instance subspaceFactorAlgebra (i : ι) : Algebra ℚ_[p] (P.Factor i) :=
  padicAlgebra (P.residueChar_eq i)
local instance subspaceFactorFinite (i : ι) : Module.Finite ℚ_[p] (P.Factor i) :=
  finite_padicAlgebra (P.residueChar_eq i)
local instance subspaceTensorFinite : Module.Finite ℚ_[p] P.Tensor :=
  Module.Finite.of_basis P.tensorBasis

theorem normalizationHaar_submodule_eq_zero (S : Submodule ℚ_[p] P.Tensor) (hS : S ≠ ⊤) :
    P.normalizationHaar (S : Set P.Tensor) = 0
lemma exists_residue_ne_zero_of_haar_pos {U : Set P.Tensor}
    (hU : 0 < P.normalizationHaar U) (m : MaximalSpectrum P.Tensor) :
    ∃ x ∈ U, Ideal.Quotient.mk m.asIdeal x ≠ 0
end
end JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1.LocalCompletion.Packet
```

Public完整类型及definition（LocalCompletionTensorBoundedHull，同生产文件精确导入）：

```lean
namespace JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1.LocalCompletion.Packet
open MeasureTheory
open scoped Classical
universe u
noncomputable section
variable {K : Type u} [Field K] [NumberField K] {p : ℕ} [Fact p.Prime]
variable {ι : Type} [Fintype ι] (P : Packet K p ι)
local instance hullFactorAlgebra (i : ι) : Algebra ℚ_[p] (P.Factor i) :=
  padicAlgebra (P.residueChar_eq i)
local instance hullFactorFinite (i : ι) : Module.Finite ℚ_[p] (P.Factor i) :=
  finite_padicAlgebra (P.residueChar_eq i)
local instance hullTensorFinite : Module.Finite ℚ_[p] P.Tensor :=
  Module.Finite.of_basis P.tensorBasis

def principalLinearEquiv (a : P.Tensor) (ha : IsUnit a) : P.Tensor ≃ₗ[ℚ_[p]] P.Tensor where
  toFun x := a * x
  invFun x := ↑ha.unit⁻¹ * x
  left_inv x := by rw [← mul_assoc, ha.val_inv_mul, one_mul]
  right_inv x := by rw [← mul_assoc, ha.mul_val_inv, one_mul]
  map_add' := mul_add a
  map_smul' r x := by rw [mul_smul_comm]

theorem principalRegion_isCompact (a : P.Tensor) (ha : IsUnit a) :
    IsCompact (P.principalRegion a)
theorem principalRegion_isOpen (a : P.Tensor) (ha : IsUnit a) :
    IsOpen (P.principalRegion a)
theorem principalRegion_haar_pos_lt_top (a : P.Tensor) (ha : IsUnit a) :
    0 < P.normalizationHaar (P.principalRegion a) ∧
      P.normalizationHaar (P.principalRegion a) < ⊤
theorem exists_least_bounded_principalHull (U : Set P.Tensor)
    (hU : Bornology.IsBounded U)
    (hcoord : ∀ I : MaximalSpectrum P.Tensor, ∃ x ∈ U, Ideal.Quotient.mk I.asIdeal x ≠ 0) :
    ∃ a : P.Tensor, IsUnit a ∧ U ⊆ P.principalRegion a ∧
      ∀ b : P.Tensor, IsUnit b → U ⊆ P.principalRegion b →
        P.principalRegion a ⊆ P.principalRegion b
theorem exists_least_positiveHaar_hull (U : Set P.Tensor)
    (hU : Bornology.IsBounded U) (hpos : 0 < P.normalizationHaar U) :
    ∃ a : P.Tensor, IsUnit a ∧ U ⊆ P.principalRegion a ∧
      IsCompact (P.principalRegion a) ∧ IsOpen (P.principalRegion a) ∧
      (0 < P.normalizationHaar (P.principalRegion a) ∧
        P.normalizationHaar (P.principalRegion a) < ⊤) ∧
      ∀ b : P.Tensor, IsUnit b → U ⊆ P.principalRegion b →
        P.principalRegion a ⊆ P.principalRegion b
end
end JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1.LocalCompletion.Packet
```

最终principalLinearEquiv定义（替代上方首轮实现记录，类型不变）：

```lean
def principalLinearEquiv (a : P.Tensor) (ha : IsUnit a) : P.Tensor ≃ₗ[ℚ_[p]] P.Tensor where
  toFun x := a * x
  invFun x := ↑ha.unit⁻¹ * x
  left_inv x := by
    change ↑ha.unit⁻¹ * (a * x) = x
    rw [← mul_assoc, ha.val_inv_mul, one_mul]
  right_inv x := by
    change a * (↑ha.unit⁻¹ * x) = x
    rw [← mul_assoc, ha.mul_val_inv, one_mul]
  map_add' := mul_add a
  map_smul' r x := by
    change a * (r • x) = r • (a * x)
    rw [mul_smul_comm]

```

Public完整类型及definition（LocalCompletionTensorChosenHull，同生产文件精确导入）：

```lean
namespace JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1.LocalCompletion.Packet
open MeasureTheory
open scoped Classical
universe u
noncomputable section
variable {K : Type u} [Field K] [NumberField K] {p : ℕ} [Fact p.Prime]
variable {ι : Type} [Fintype ι] (P : Packet K p ι)

def hullMultiplier (U : Set P.Tensor) (hU : Bornology.IsBounded U)
    (hpos : 0 < P.normalizationHaar U) : P.Tensor :=
  (P.exists_least_positiveHaar_hull U hU hpos).choose

def leastHull (U : Set P.Tensor) (hU : Bornology.IsBounded U)
    (hpos : 0 < P.normalizationHaar U) : Set P.Tensor :=
  P.principalRegion (P.hullMultiplier U hU hpos)

theorem hullMultiplier_isUnit (U : Set P.Tensor) (hU : Bornology.IsBounded U)
    (hpos : 0 < P.normalizationHaar U) : IsUnit (P.hullMultiplier U hU hpos)
theorem subset_leastHull (U : Set P.Tensor) (hU : Bornology.IsBounded U)
    (hpos : 0 < P.normalizationHaar U) : U ⊆ P.leastHull U hU hpos
theorem leastHull_isCompact (U : Set P.Tensor) (hU : Bornology.IsBounded U)
    (hpos : 0 < P.normalizationHaar U) : IsCompact (P.leastHull U hU hpos)
theorem leastHull_isOpen (U : Set P.Tensor) (hU : Bornology.IsBounded U)
    (hpos : 0 < P.normalizationHaar U) : IsOpen (P.leastHull U hU hpos)
theorem leastHull_haar_pos_lt_top (U : Set P.Tensor) (hU : Bornology.IsBounded U)
    (hpos : 0 < P.normalizationHaar U) :
    0 < P.normalizationHaar (P.leastHull U hU hpos) ∧
      P.normalizationHaar (P.leastHull U hU hpos) < ⊤
theorem leastHull_le_principalRegion (U : Set P.Tensor) (hU : Bornology.IsBounded U)
    (hpos : 0 < P.normalizationHaar U) (a : P.Tensor) (ha : IsUnit a)
    (hUa : U ⊆ P.principalRegion a) : P.leastHull U hU hpos ⊆ P.principalRegion a
theorem leastHull_mono {U V : Set P.Tensor}
    (hU : Bornology.IsBounded U) (hUpos : 0 < P.normalizationHaar U)
    (hV : Bornology.IsBounded V) (hVpos : 0 < P.normalizationHaar V) (hUV : U ⊆ V) :
    P.leastHull U hU hUpos ⊆ P.leastHull V hV hVpos
theorem leastHull_eq_of_principal_minimal (U : Set P.Tensor) (hU : Bornology.IsBounded U)
    (hpos : 0 < P.normalizationHaar U) (a : P.Tensor) (ha : IsUnit a)
    (hUa : U ⊆ P.principalRegion a)
    (hmin : ∀ b : P.Tensor, IsUnit b → U ⊆ P.principalRegion b →
      P.principalRegion a ⊆ P.principalRegion b) :
    P.leastHull U hU hpos = P.principalRegion a
theorem leastHull_principalRegion (a : P.Tensor) (ha : IsUnit a) :
    P.leastHull (P.principalRegion a) (P.principalRegion_isCompact a ha).isBounded
      (P.principalRegion_haar_pos_lt_top a ha).1 = P.principalRegion a
end
end JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1.LocalCompletion.Packet
```

Public完整类型及definition（LocalCompletionTensorScalarHullVolume，同生产文件精确导入）：

```lean
namespace JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1.LocalCompletion.Packet
open MeasureTheory
open scoped Classical
universe u
noncomputable section
variable {K : Type u} [Field K] [NumberField K] {p : ℕ} [Fact p.Prime]
variable {ι : Type} [Fintype ι] (P : Packet K p ι)
local instance scalarHullIntegralModule : Module ℤ_[p] P.Tensor :=
  Module.compHom P.Tensor (algebraMap ℤ_[p] ℚ_[p])
variable (z : ∀ i, P.Factor i) (hz : ∀ i, z i ≠ 0)

theorem scalarNormalization_eq_principalRegion :
    (P.scalarNormalization z hz : Set P.Tensor) = P.principalRegion (P.pure z)
include hz in
theorem scalarLattice_isCompact_of_nonzero : IsCompact (P.scalarLattice z : Set P.Tensor)
include hz in
theorem scalarLattice_normalizationHaar_pos : 0 < P.normalizationHaar (P.scalarLattice z)
theorem scalarNormalization_subset_of_scalarLattice_subset (a : P.Tensor)
    (hregion : (P.scalarLattice z : Set P.Tensor) ⊆ P.principalRegion a) :
    (P.scalarNormalization z hz : Set P.Tensor) ⊆ P.principalRegion a
theorem leastHull_scalarLattice :
    P.leastHull (P.scalarLattice z) (P.scalarLattice_isCompact_of_nonzero z hz).isBounded
      (P.scalarLattice_normalizationHaar_pos z hz) = P.scalarNormalization z hz
theorem normalizationDegreeVolume_mono {U V : Set P.Tensor}
    (hUV : U ⊆ V) (hV : P.normalizationHaar V ≠ ⊤) :
    P.normalizationDegreeVolume U ≤ P.normalizationDegreeVolume V
include hz in
theorem degreeVolume_scalarLattice_le_leastHullVolume (U : Set P.Tensor)
    (hU : Bornology.IsBounded U) (hpos : 0 < P.normalizationHaar U)
    (hregion : (P.scalarLattice z : Set P.Tensor) ⊆ U) :
    P.degreeVolume (P.scalarLattice z) ≤ P.normalizationDegreeVolume (P.leastHull U hU hpos)
end
end JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1.LocalCompletion.Packet
```

Public完整类型及definition（IutInitialThetaFixedModuliHullVolume，同生产文件精确导入）：

```lean
namespace JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1.FixedModuliThetaHull
open JoshiTeich.Prologue
open scoped Classical
universe u
noncomputable section
variable {AG : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.AnabelianGeometry.{u}}
variable {TG : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.TemperedGeometry AG}
variable (D : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.InitialThetaData AG TG)
variable (v : iutInitialThetaOddModSupportFinset D)
variable {ι : Type} [Fintype ι]
variable (σ : ι → (iutInitialThetaOddSupportTorsionQRootSource D).badFinset)
variable (hσ : ∀ i, Ideal.LiesOver (σ i).1.maximalIdeal.asIdeal v.1.maximalIdeal.asIdeal)
local instance hullPrime : Fact (Arithmetic.residueChar v.1).Prime :=
  ⟨Arithmetic.residueChar_prime v.1⟩
local instance actualHullIntegralModule : Module ℤ_[Arithmetic.residueChar v.1]
    (FixedModuliThetaTensor.packet D v σ hσ).Tensor :=
  Module.compHom (FixedModuliThetaTensor.packet D v σ hσ).Tensor
    (algebraMap ℤ_[Arithmetic.residueChar v.1] ℚ_[Arithmetic.residueChar v.1])
variable (U : Set (FixedModuliThetaTensor.packet D v σ hσ).Tensor)
variable (hU : Bornology.IsBounded U)
variable (hpos : 0 < (FixedModuliThetaTensor.packet D v σ hσ).normalizationHaar U)

def volume : ℝ :=
  (FixedModuliThetaTensor.packet D v σ hσ).normalizationDegreeVolume
    ((FixedModuliThetaTensor.packet D v σ hσ).leastHull U hU hpos) ^ Arithmetic.localDeg v.1

theorem relativeFactor_pow_le_volume
    (hregion : (FixedModuliThetaTensor.region D v σ hσ :
      Set (FixedModuliThetaTensor.packet D v σ hσ).Tensor) ⊆ U) :
    iutInitialThetaRelativeQRootFactor D v ^ Fintype.card ι ≤ volume D v σ hσ U hU hpos
end
end JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1.FixedModuliThetaHull
```

```lean
namespace JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1.LocalCompletion.Packet
open MeasureTheory
universe u
noncomputable section
variable {K : Type u} [Field K] [NumberField K] {p : ℕ} [Fact p.Prime]
variable {ι : Type} [Fintype ι] (P : Packet K p ι)

theorem normalizationDegreeVolume_le_one (S : Set P.Tensor) (hS : S ⊆ P.normalization) :
    P.normalizationDegreeVolume S ≤ 1
theorem leastHull_subset_normalization (U : Set P.Tensor) (hU : Bornology.IsBounded U)
    (hpos : 0 < P.normalizationHaar U) (hUn : U ⊆ P.normalization) :
    P.leastHull U hU hpos ⊆ P.normalization
theorem leastHull_normalizationDegreeVolume_pos (U : Set P.Tensor)
    (hU : Bornology.IsBounded U) (hpos : 0 < P.normalizationHaar U) :
    0 < P.normalizationDegreeVolume (P.leastHull U hU hpos)
end
end JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1.LocalCompletion.Packet
```

Public完整类型及definition（IutInitialThetaVBadChosenHullVolume，同生产文件精确导入）：

```lean
namespace JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1.VBadThetaHull
open JoshiTeich.Prologue
open scoped BigOperators Classical
universe u
noncomputable section
variable {AG : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.AnabelianGeometry.{u}}
variable {TG : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.TemperedGeometry AG}
variable (D : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.InitialThetaData AG TG) (n : ℕ)
variable (σ : iutInitialThetaVBadModuliFinset D → Fin n →
  (iutInitialThetaOddSupportTorsionQRootSource D).badFinset)
variable (hσ : ∀ v i, Ideal.LiesOver (σ v i).1.maximalIdeal.asIdeal v.1.maximalIdeal.asIdeal)
local instance globalHullPrime (v : iutInitialThetaVBadModuliFinset D) :
    Fact (Arithmetic.residueChar v.1).Prime := ⟨Arithmetic.residueChar_prime v.1⟩

abbrev packet (v : iutInitialThetaVBadModuliFinset D) :=
  FixedModuliThetaTensor.packet D (iutInitialThetaVBadModuliAt D v) (σ v) (hσ v)

local instance globalHullIntegralModule (v : iutInitialThetaVBadModuliFinset D) :
    Module ℤ_[Arithmetic.residueChar v.1] (packet D n σ hσ v).Tensor :=
  Module.compHom (packet D n σ hσ v).Tensor
    (algebraMap ℤ_[Arithmetic.residueChar v.1] ℚ_[Arithmetic.residueChar v.1])
variable (U : ∀ v : iutInitialThetaVBadModuliFinset D, Set (packet D n σ hσ v).Tensor)
variable (hU : ∀ v, Bornology.IsBounded (U v))
variable (hpos : ∀ v, 0 < (packet D n σ hσ v).normalizationHaar (U v))

def volume : ℝ :=
  (∏ v : iutInitialThetaVBadModuliFinset D,
    (packet D n σ hσ v).normalizationDegreeVolume
      ((packet D n σ hσ v).leastHull (U v) (hU v) (hpos v)) ^ Arithmetic.localDeg v.1) ^
    (1 / (Module.finrank ℚ ↥(JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.fieldOfModuli D.F D.E) : ℝ))

theorem volume_pos : 0 < volume D n σ hσ U hU hpos
theorem normalizedRootProduct_pow_le_volume
    (hregion : ∀ v, (FixedModuliThetaTensor.region D (iutInitialThetaVBadModuliAt D v)
      (σ v) (hσ v) : Set (packet D n σ hσ v).Tensor) ⊆ U v) :
    iutInitialThetaNormalizedVBadRelativeQRootProduct D ^ n ≤ volume D n σ hσ U hU hpos
theorem volume_le_one (hunit : ∀ v, U v ⊆ (packet D n σ hσ v).normalization) :
    volume D n σ hσ U hU hpos ≤ 1
theorem absLog_volume_le_mul_referenceAbsLogQ
    (hregion : ∀ v, (FixedModuliThetaTensor.region D (iutInitialThetaVBadModuliAt D v)
      (σ v) (hσ v) : Set (packet D n σ hσ v).Tensor) ⊆ U v)
    (hunit : ∀ v, U v ⊆ (packet D n σ hσ v).normalization) :
    |Real.log (volume D n σ hσ U hU hpos)| ≤ (n : ℝ) *
      JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.QPilotData.absLogQ (IutTorsionQRootSource.iutInitialThetaQPilotData D)
theorem negAverageAbsLog_volume_ge_referenceLhs (hn : 0 < n)
    (hregion : ∀ v, (FixedModuliThetaTensor.region D (iutInitialThetaVBadModuliAt D v)
      (σ v) (hσ v) : Set (packet D n σ hσ v).Tensor) ⊆ U v)
    (hunit : ∀ v, U v ⊆ (packet D n σ hσ v).normalization) :
    -(1 / (n : ℝ)) * |Real.log (volume D n σ hσ U hU hpos)| ≥
      -JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.QPilotData.absLogQ (IutTorsionQRootSource.iutInitialThetaQPilotData D)
end
end JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1.VBadThetaHull
```

Public完整类型及definition（LocalCompletionTensorHullOrderAudit，生产叶子的精确imports如文件所示）：

```lean
namespace JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1.LocalCompletion.Packet
open MeasureTheory
universe u
noncomputable section
variable {K : Type u} [Field K] [NumberField K] {p : ℕ} [Fact p.Prime]
variable {ι : Type} [Fintype ι] (P : Packet K p ι)
local instance orderAuditIntegralModule : Module ℤ_[p] P.Tensor :=
  Module.compHom P.Tensor (algebraMap ℤ_[p] ℚ_[p])

omit [Fintype ι] in
theorem integralHull_submodule (L : Submodule ℤ_[p] P.Tensor) :
    P.integralHull (L : Set P.Tensor) = L

variable (z : ∀ i, P.Factor i) (hz : ∀ i, z i ≠ 0)

theorem scalarNormalization_eq_scalarLattice_iff :
    P.scalarNormalization z hz = P.scalarLattice z ↔
      P.normalization.toSubmodule = P.unitLattice

include hz in
theorem leastHull_scalarLattice_eq_integralHull_iff
    (hU : Bornology.IsBounded (P.scalarLattice z : Set P.Tensor))
    (hpos : 0 < P.normalizationHaar (P.scalarLattice z)) :
    P.leastHull (P.scalarLattice z) hU hpos =
        (P.integralHull (P.scalarLattice z) : Set P.Tensor) ↔
      P.normalization.toSubmodule = P.unitLattice

theorem normalizationMass_eq_one_iff :
    P.normalizationMass = 1 ↔ P.normalization.toSubmodule = P.unitLattice

theorem normalizationHaar_scalarNormalization_eq_mass_mul_scalarLattice :
    P.normalizationHaar (P.scalarNormalization z hz) =
      P.normalizationMass * P.normalizationHaar (P.scalarLattice z)

include hz in
theorem leastHull_scalarLattice_ne_integralHull_of_order_ne
    (hU : Bornology.IsBounded (P.scalarLattice z : Set P.Tensor))
    (hpos : 0 < P.normalizationHaar (P.scalarLattice z))
    (hne : P.normalization.toSubmodule ≠ P.unitLattice) :
    P.leastHull (P.scalarLattice z) hU hpos ≠
      (P.integralHull (P.scalarLattice z) : Set P.Tensor)

end
end JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1.LocalCompletion.Packet
```


Public完整类型及definition（IutInitialThetaFixedModuliHullOrderAudit，生产叶子的精确imports如文件所示）：

```lean
namespace JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1.FixedModuliThetaHullOrder
open JoshiTeich.Prologue
open scoped Classical
universe u
noncomputable section
variable {AG : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.AnabelianGeometry.{u}}
variable {TG : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.TemperedGeometry AG}
variable (D : JoshiTeich.ReferenceIut.InitialThetaData AG TG)
variable (v : iutInitialThetaOddModSupportFinset D)
variable {ι : Type} [Fintype ι]
variable (σ : ι → (iutInitialThetaOddSupportTorsionQRootSource D).badFinset)
variable (hσ : ∀ i, Ideal.LiesOver (σ i).1.maximalIdeal.asIdeal v.1.maximalIdeal.asIdeal)
local instance actualOrderPrime : Fact (Arithmetic.residueChar v.1).Prime :=
  ⟨Arithmetic.residueChar_prime v.1⟩
local instance actualOrderIntegralModule : Module ℤ_[Arithmetic.residueChar v.1]
    (FixedModuliThetaTensor.packet D v σ hσ).Tensor :=
  Module.compHom (FixedModuliThetaTensor.packet D v σ hσ).Tensor
    (algebraMap ℤ_[Arithmetic.residueChar v.1] ℚ_[Arithmetic.residueChar v.1])

theorem region_isCompact : IsCompact (FixedModuliThetaTensor.region D v σ hσ :
    Set (FixedModuliThetaTensor.packet D v σ hσ).Tensor)

theorem region_normalizationHaar_pos :
    0 < (FixedModuliThetaTensor.packet D v σ hσ).normalizationHaar
      (FixedModuliThetaTensor.region D v σ hσ)

def regionHull : Set (FixedModuliThetaTensor.packet D v σ hσ).Tensor :=
  (FixedModuliThetaTensor.packet D v σ hσ).leastHull
    (FixedModuliThetaTensor.region D v σ hσ) (region_isCompact D v σ hσ).isBounded
    (region_normalizationHaar_pos D v σ hσ)

theorem regionHull_eq_integralHull_iff_order_eq :
    regionHull D v σ hσ =
        ((FixedModuliThetaTensor.packet D v σ hσ).integralHull
          (FixedModuliThetaTensor.region D v σ hσ) :
          Set (FixedModuliThetaTensor.packet D v σ hσ).Tensor) ↔
      (FixedModuliThetaTensor.packet D v σ hσ).normalization.toSubmodule =
        (FixedModuliThetaTensor.packet D v σ hσ).unitLattice

theorem regionHull_eq_integralHull_iff_mass_eq_one :
    regionHull D v σ hσ =
        ((FixedModuliThetaTensor.packet D v σ hσ).integralHull
          (FixedModuliThetaTensor.region D v σ hσ) :
          Set (FixedModuliThetaTensor.packet D v σ hσ).Tensor) ↔
      (FixedModuliThetaTensor.packet D v σ hσ).normalizationMass = 1

theorem regionHull_ne_integralHull_of_mass_ne_one
    (hne : (FixedModuliThetaTensor.packet D v σ hσ).normalizationMass ≠ 1) :
    regionHull D v σ hσ ≠
      ((FixedModuliThetaTensor.packet D v σ hσ).integralHull
        (FixedModuliThetaTensor.region D v σ hσ) :
        Set (FixedModuliThetaTensor.packet D v σ hσ).Tensor)

end
end JoshiTeich.Act1Scene1.FixedModuliThetaHullOrder
```
